Complete Master A–Z Syllabus

तन्त्र और साधना मार्ग

Tantra aur Sadhana Marg

Basic se advanced tak: darshan, Agama, mantra, yantra, puja, yoga, devata-upasana, granth, parampara aur adhunik shodh. 36 adhyay, 262 vishay. Har vishay ka seedha jawab, uska kaaran aur shastriya aadhar.

Adhyay 1 se shuru kareinPehle zaroori correctionsStudy roadmap

0 / 36 adhyay padhe

Is website ko kaise padhein

Har adhyay mein syllabus ka har vishay ek alag khand hai. Khand ka sheershak syllabus ki wahi line hai, aur uske neeche seedha jawab milta hai.

Neele dabbe mein Kyun / Aadhar hai, yaani us baat ka kaaran. Peele dabbe mein Dhyan rakhein hai, jahan galat-fehmi ya jokhim ho sakta hai. Chhote mono-font mein Pramana hai, yaani baat kis granth ya vidwan ke aadhar par kahi gayi.

Upar search box mein koi bhi shabd likhein, jaise “spanda” ya “nyasa”. Keyboard par / dabane se bhi search khulta hai. Adhyay ke end mein “padh liya” dabayein; progress aapke browser mein save hota hai.

Scope aur seema

Yeh ek vistarit, source-aware study map hai. Yeh kisi ek granth ki copy nahi hai aur duniya ke har tantrik manuscript ka catalogue bhi nahi. Tantra ek single, uniform system nahi: Shaiva, Shakta, Vaishnava, Buddhist, Jain aur regional paramparaon mein farq milta hai. Yeh mukhyatah gyaan, textual study aur aitihasik samajh ke liye hai, diksha-adharit sadhana ka practical manual nahi. Original syllabus ke title mein 32 kshetra likha tha, par uske andar 36 adhyay hain; yahan sabhi 36 liye gaye hain.

Yeh site seekhne aur adhyayan ke liye hai. Diksha-yukt ya lineage-vishesh sadhana ke liye yogya guru aur sampraday ka sahara lein. Jahan vidwan ek-mat nahi hain, wahan ‘lagbhag’ ya ‘kai vidwan’ likha gaya hai.

Shuruaat

Maujooda list ke zaroori corrections

Tantra ke baare mein kuch baatein aam taur par ek-jaisi maan li jaati hain. Neeche har daave ke saamne uski behtar samajh aur uska kaaran diya hai.

Shaiva 28, Shakta 64, Vaishnava 108

Yeh sankhyaen kuch paramparik classifications mein milti hain. Inhe sabhi traditions ki universal, final ginti na maanein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Shaiva Siddhanta ke 28 Agama ek jaani-maani paramparik ginti hai, aur Pancharatra ke liye 108 Samhita ka zikr milta hai. Shakta ke 64 Tantra ka zikr Saundarya Lahari ke 31ve shloka mein aata hai. Par granthon ki ginti sampraday aur kaal ke hisaab se badalti hai, aur kai naam sirf naam se jaane jaate hain, unke text maujood nahi.

Aage padheinAdhyay 3 Adhyay 19

Har Tantra mein saat Agama-lakshan hote hain

Kuch granthon mein diya lakshan-samuh har Tantra par apne-aap laagu nahi hota.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ek prasiddh paramparik shloka Agama ke saat lakshan ginata hai: srishti, pralaya, devata-archana, sarva-sadhana, purashcharana, shatkarma aur dhyana-yoga. Yeh ek aadarsh-roop vargikaran hai. Vastav mein kai granth sirf kuch hi vishayon par kendrit hain, jaise koi mukhya roop se darshan par aur koi sirf mandir-puja par.

Aage padheinAdhyay 1

Dakshinachara, Vamachara aur Kaulachara ka ek hi nishchit arth hai

Arth aur paraspar sambandh granth aur lineage ke hisaab se badal sakte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kularnava Tantra saat achar ko uchch-krama mein ginata hai (Veda se Kaula tak), jabki dusre granth aur sampraday in shabdon ko alag tarah se istemal karte hain. 'Vama' ka arth 'bura' nahi, aur 'dakshina' ka arth hamesha 'sahi' nahi hota.

Aage padheinAdhyay 28

Har chakra/nadi ka ek hi fixed map hai

Chakra, nadi, bija, varna aur devata ke models granthon mein alag ho sakte hain. Yeh paramparik subtle-body models hain, adhunik anatomy ka seedha map nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Lokpriya saat-chakra naksha mukhya roop se Shat-chakra-nirupana (lagbhag 1577 ee.) par aadharit hai, jisme chhah chakra aur sahasrara aate hain. Kai Kaula, Shaiva aur Kubjika granth alag kendra aur alag ginti dete hain. Rang aur bimari wali sarani aadhunik hai.

Aage padheinAdhyay 20 Adhyay 21

Har Panchamakara ka arth ek jaisa hai

Literal, ritual, symbolic aur interpretive readings parampara-vishesh ke anusaar alag hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kuch granth substitute (doodh, shahad jaise) ka vidhan karte hain, kuch aantarik yogic arth dete hain, aur Shaiva Siddhanta tatha Vaishnava Pancharatra jaise sampraday in sabka pratishedh karte hain. Isliye 'Panchamakara yaani X' jaisa ek vaakya galat hoga.

Aage padheinAdhyay 28

Mahanirvana Tantra ko nishchit roop se bahut prachin kehna

Iske maujooda text-roop ki dating par scholarly charcha hai. Kai aadhunik vidwan ise 18vi sadi ke Bengal se jodte hain. Dating ko caveat ke saath likhna chahiye.

Kyun / Aadhar

Granth mein grihastha-dharm, samskara aur samajik vyavastha par vistrit vidhan hain, jo madhyakaleen Tantra se alag swar rakhte hain. Isi karan vidwan iske kaal par savdhani se baat karte hain. Isliye 'hazaaron saal puraana' jaise daave se bachein.

Aage padheinAdhyay 4 Adhyay 36

Har mantra ke sabhi ang hote hain

Rishi, chhanda, devata, bija, shakti, keelaka aur viniyoga ka prayog mantra-parampara ke hisaab se badalta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kai mantra-paddhatiyon mein sat ang batate hain, par har mantra ke saath saare ang nahi milte. Kuch mein sirf rishi, chhanda aur devata aate hain, aur kuch mein sirf viniyoga.

Aage padheinAdhyay 11

Har Hatha Yoga text Tantra-granth hai

Kuch Hatha Yoga texts tantrik traditions se sambandhit hain, lekin har yoga text ko apne-aap Tantrashastra nahi kaha ja sakta.

Kyun / Aadhar

Hatha Yoga ka Nath aur tantrik paramparaon se gehra sambandh hai, par Hatha Yoga Pradipika, Gheranda Samhita aur Shiva Samhita ke apne alag vishay, swar aur kaal hain, aur Patanjali ka Yogasutra to alag dhara ka hai.

Aage padheinAdhyay 22 Adhyay 35

Sabhi Tantra granthon mein sabhi topics milte hain

Kuch darshanik, kuch ritual, kuch mandir-Agama, kuch mantra ya yoga-kendrit hote hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Udaharan: Ishvarapratyabhijna Karika mukhya roop se darshan hai, Tantrasamuccaya mandir-ritual ka granth hai, aur Sharada Tilaka mantra aur puja par kendrit hai. Kisi ek granth se 'sab Tantra aisa hi hai' ka nishkarsh nahi nikalna chahiye.

Aage padheinAdhyay 1 Adhyay 4

Shuruaat

Basic se advanced study roadmap

Paanch phase mein padhai ka kram. Samay anumaan hai; apni gati se chalein aur har phase ke prashn khud se poochkar hi aage badhein.

  1. 1

    Foundation

    Pehle 4 se 6 hafte

    Tantra terminology, Hindu philosophical background, Sanskrit pronunciation, text genres, basic worship concepts aur guru-parampara ka role samjhein. Daily study notebook shuru karein.

    Is phase mein kya karein
    • Adhyay 1 se 4 padhein, aur har naya shabd Shabdakosh mein dekhein.
    • Ek notebook mein roz ka ek sawal aur ek naya shabd likhein.
    • Devanagari ke saath Sanskrit ke varn aur sandhi ke basic niyam dekhein.
    • Adhyay 9 aur 10 se guru aur diksha ke red flags yaad rakhein.
    Is website ke adhyay
    Adhyay 1 Adhyay 2 Adhyay 3 Adhyay 4 Adhyay 9 Adhyay 10
    Aage badhne se pehle khud se poochein
    • Kya main Tantra, Agama, Yamala aur Kalpa mein antar bata sakta hoon?
    • Kya main mool granth, tika aur modern kitab ko alag pehchan leta hoon?
    • Kya main kam se kam teen sampradayon ke naam aur ek-ek pehchaan bata sakta hoon?
  2. 2

    Darshan aur textual literacy

    Agle 2 se 3 mahine

    Shiva–Shakti tattva, 36 tattva, mala, upaya, Spanda, Pratyabhijna aur mantra theory padhein. The Hindu Tantric World aur Tantra Illuminated ko primary texts ke saath compare karein.

    Is phase mein kya karein
    • Adhyay 5 se 8 par kam se kam do baar jaayein.
    • Shiva Sutra ya Pratyabhijna Hridayam jaise chhote granth ka ek anuvad aur ek tika saath padhein.
    • Mantra-shastra (adhyay 11 se 14) ko shabdavali ke roop mein samjhein, vidhi ke roop mein nahi.
    Is website ke adhyay
    Adhyay 5 Adhyay 6 Adhyay 7 Adhyay 8 Adhyay 11 Adhyay 12 Adhyay 13 Adhyay 14
    Aage badhne se pehle khud se poochein
    • Kya main 36 tattva ka kram aur paanch kanchuka bina dekhe bata sakta hoon?
    • Kya main teen mala aur chaar upaya samjha sakta hoon?
    • Kya main vani ke chaar star aur 50 matrika ka sambandh bata sakta hoon?
  3. 3

    Primary texts

    Agle 3 se 4 mahine

    Ek track chunein: Kashmir Shaivism, Shakta/Kaula, Shri Vidya ya Agama/temple traditions. Chhote text se shuru karke commentary aur glossary ke saath padhein.

    Is phase mein kya karein
    • Granth suchi mein apne track ke granth dekhein aur ek chhota granth chunein.
    • Mool Sanskrit, transliteration, ek anuvad aur ek tika ko saath rakh kar ek shloka par ek page ka note likhein.
    • Adhyay 15 se 27 mein se apne track ke adhyay dobara padhein.
    Is website ke adhyay
    Adhyay 15 Adhyay 16 Adhyay 17 Adhyay 18 Adhyay 19 Adhyay 20 Adhyay 21 Adhyay 22 Adhyay 23 Adhyay 24 Adhyay 25 Adhyay 26 Adhyay 27
    Aage badhne se pehle khud se poochein
    • Kya main apne chune hue granth ke lekhak, kaal aur sampraday bata sakta hoon, aur kya meri jaankari ke srot saaf hain?
    • Kya main ek shloka ka padaccheda aur anvaya kar sakta hoon?
  4. 4

    Comparative study

    Agle 3 se 6 mahine

    Do granthon mein ek hi concept compare karein: mantra, tattva, guru, initiation, puja ya moksha. Text passages, terminology, commentary aur dating note karein.

    Is phase mein kya karein
    • Ek concept chunein, jaise 'diksha' ya 'mukti', aur do alag sampradayon ke granthon se passage nikaalein.
    • Adhyay 33 aur 34 se Buddhist aur Jain parampara ke saath ek tulna-table banayein.
    • Adhyay 28 aur 29 ko sirf aitihasik, granthiya aur naitik drishti se padhein.
    Is website ke adhyay
    Adhyay 28 Adhyay 29 Adhyay 30 Adhyay 31 Adhyay 32 Adhyay 33 Adhyay 34 Adhyay 35
    Aage badhne se pehle khud se poochein
    • Kya meri tulna mein samanta aur antar dono hain, aur har baat ke saath granth ka hawala hai?
    • Kya main dating aur authorship ke sawal par 'pata nahi' kehne ko tayyar hoon jahan saboot nahi hai?
  5. 5

    Advanced specialization

    Long-term

    Sanskrit text-reading, manuscript variants, specialist commentaries aur research publications par kaam karein. Diksha-yukt ya lineage-specific practice ko independent reading se automatic authorization na samjhein.

    Is phase mein kya karein
    • Kisi ek granth ka critical edition aur manuscript-jaankari dhoondhein, aur do sanskaran ke pathantara ki tulna karein.
    • Adhyay 36 ki vidwan-suchi mein se apne kshetra ke vidwan ke shodh-patra padhein.
    • Jeevit parampara se judna chahein to ek yogya guru ko dhairya aur viveka se parakhein.
    Is website ke adhyay
    Adhyay 4 Adhyay 36
    Aage badhne se pehle khud se poochein
    • Kya main kisi granth ke paath-bhed ko apparatus ke saath padh sakta hoon?
    • Kya main academic aur practitioner drishtikon ke beech santulan rakh paata hoon?

PART I · Neev aur parampara

01

Tantra ka parichay aur terminology

तन्त्र का परिचय और शब्दावली

Is adhyay mein Tantra shabd ka arth, usse jude granth-prakar (Agama, Yamala, Kalpa aadi) aur sabse badi galatfehmiyan saaf hongi. Baaki poori site isi neev par khadi hai.

Basic8 vishay

Tantra shabd ka arth, vyutpatti aur prayog.

Tantra shabd tan dhatu se bana hai, jiska arth hai phailana ya bunana. Isliye iska sabse purana arth bunai ka dhancha, yaani loom ya tana-bana hai, aur yeh arth Vedic bhasha mein bhi milta hai. Baad mein shabd ka arth badal kar mool dhancha, siddhant ya kisi kaam ki paddhati ho gaya (jaise rajya-tantra).

Sampraday ke andar ek paramparik vyakhya kehti hai ki jo granth gyaan ka vistar kare (tan) aur sadhak ki raksha kare (tra), woh Tantra hai. Yeh vyakhya Kamika Agama jaise granthon ke naam se aksar uddhrit hoti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Shabd ka asli arth jaanna isliye zaroori hai ki 19vi sadi ke Yurop ne 'Tantrism' ko ek gupt aur vichitra cheez bana diya. Padoux jaise vidwan batate hain ki paramparik tantrik khud apne liye 'Tantrism' shabd nahi bolte the.

PramanaPadoux, The Hindu Tantric World (parichay); Goudriaan aur Gupta, Hindu Tantric and Sakta Literature; Kamika Agama ki paramparik vyakhya.

Tantra, Agama, Nigama, Samhita, Yamala, Damara aur Kalpa mein antar.

ShabdAam arthUdaharan
TantraVishesh granth-prakar jisme mantra, puja, yoga aur darshan milte hain; aksar Shiva aur Devi ke sanvad ke roop meinKularnava Tantra
Agama'Aaya hua' (paramparaa se prapt); Shaiva Siddhanta ke 28 Agama aur Vaishnava Agama; mandir-puja par vishesh dhyanKamika Agama
NigamaVeda ke liye bhi prayukt; Tantra mein kabhi Agama (Shiva se Devi) ke saamne Devi se Shiva ke sanvad-roop ko bhi kehte hainShakta granthon ke sanvad
SamhitaSangrah; Vaishnava Pancharatra aur Vaikhanasa granth aksar Samhita kehlate hainAhirbudhnya Samhita
Yamala'Yugal' ya jodi; Shiva-Shakti ke yugal-sanvad wale granthRudrayamala
DamaraEk tantra-varg ka naam, jise aam taur par vyavharik aur laukik prayogon wali rachnaon se joda jaata hai; is varg ki seema par granthon mein ekmat nahiIs naam ke granth-varg ke liye Goudriaan aur Gupta ka sandarbh dekhein
KalpaRitual ke kram aur vidhi ka sutra-roop granthParashurama Kalpasutra
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh shabd alag-alag kaal aur sampradayon mein badalte arth ke saath chalte rahe. Isiliye ek granth ke naam se uska varg turant tay nahi hota.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh dabbe sakht diwaren nahi hain. Kai granth ek se zyada shreni mein aate hain aur vidwan vargikaran mein ek-mat nahi hote. Kisi bhi granth ke liye uske apne sampraday ka istemal dekhein.

Tantra-shastra, mantra-shastra, agama-shastra aur ritual manuals ka sambandh.

Tantra-shastra sabse badi chhatri hai. Mantra-shastra uska woh ang hai jo mantra ki rachna, devata, nyasa aur japa batata hai. Agama-shastra mukhyatah mandir ki puja, murti aur theology par kendrit hai. Ritual manual (paddhati, prayoga) kisi mool granth ke vidhan ko krama se jod kar vyavharik roop deta hai.

Isko ek seedhi ki tarah dekhein: mool Tantra ya Agama batata hai kya aur kyun, paddhati batati hai kaise aur kis kram mein, aur upasak uska vyavhar karta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki ek hi puja ke liye mool granth aur uski paddhati mein thoda farq kyun milta hai. Paddhati usi sampraday ki rozmarra ki zarurat ke hisaab se sankshipt ya vyavasthit hoti hai.

PramanaIsanasivagurudevapaddhati (Shaiva Siddhanta ki ek bada paddhati-sangrah); Tantrasamuccaya (Kerala ka mandir-ritual granth).

Shastra, paddhati, prayoga, tika, bhashya, nibandha aur sangraha.

ShabdMatlabPehchaan ka tarika
ShastraKisi vishay ke siddhant aur niyamMool granth
PaddhatiKrama se likhi gayi puja ya ritual ki vidhiHar charan ek ke baad ek
PrayogaVidhi ka vyavharik roop, 'kaise karein'Sankshipt, kriya-kendrit
TikaMool ki pankti-dar vyakhyaMool ka ansh uddhrit karke arth
BhashyaGahri vyakhya, aksar sutra parTark aur khandan-mandan
NibandhaKai granthon ke vachan ek vishay par jod kar likha gaya digestAnek granthon ke uddharan
SangrahaSankalan, anthologyAlag-alag sthanon ke ansh ek jagah
Kyun / Aadhar

Padhne se pehle yeh jaan lena madad karta hai ki sam-mukh kitab ka darja kya hai. Tika mool granth se baad ka hota hai aur lekhak ka apna drishtikon rakhta hai.

PramanaUdaharan: Jayaratha ki tika Tantraloka par; Bhaskararaya ka Setubandha Nityashodashikarnava par.

Mool granth, commentary, oral teaching aur modern explanatory books ka farq.

Chaar star hote hain. Mool granth lekhak ya parampara ka original Sanskrit text hai. Commentary baad ke acharya ki sampraday ke andar rakhi gayi vyakhya hai. Oral teaching guru se milti hai: woh uchcharan, vidhi ke vistar aur anubhav ki baatein jo aksar likhi nahi gayi. Modern kitabein aaj ke lekhak ka anuvad ya saralikaran hoti hain, jo upyogi to hain par lekhak ki ruchi aur samajh se rangi ho sakti hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ek hi shloka par alag tikakar alag arth dete hain. Isliye Abhinavagupta ya Bhaskararaya ki baat ko seedha mool granth ki baat kehna galat hoga. Padhte samay poochhein: yeh baat mool mein hai, tika mein hai, guru-vani hai, ya modern lekhak ki vyakhya hai?

Dhyan rakhein

Tantra mein guru-mukh paramparaa ka vishesh sthan hai, isliye kai sampraday sirf kitab se padhi gayi vidhi ko adhoora maante hain. Yeh site sirf gyaan aur adhyayan ke liye hai.

Hindu Tantra, Buddhist Tantra, Jain tantric practices aur modern Neo-Tantra.

Hindu Tantra mein Shaiva, Shakta, Vaishnava (Pancharatra aur Vaikhanasa), Saura aur Ganapatya dharayein aati hain. Buddhist Tantra, yaani Vajrayana, apne alag tantra-granthon par chalta hai (Guhyasamaja, Hevajra, Kalachakra) aur Tibet, Nepal aur East Asia mein phaila. Jain paramparaon mein mantra, yantra aur yaksha-yakshini upasana ahimsa ke dayre mein milti hai (Padmavati, Jvalamalini).

Neo-Tantra 20vi sadi ke baad mukhyatah Pashchim mein bani ek aadhunik wellness-aur-spirituality lehar hai. Yeh aksar 'sacred sexuality' par kendrit hoti hai aur paramparik granthon se seedha sambandh nahi rakhti.

Kyun / Aadhar

Neo-Tantra ko paramparik Tantra se mila dene par sabse badi galatfehmi paida hoti hai. Hugh Urban aur Julian Strube ki kitabein is aadhunik itihas ko samjhati hain.

PramanaUrban, Tantra: Sex, Secrecy, Politics, and Power in the Study of Religion; Strube, Global Tantra.

Shruti, Smriti, Purana, Agama aur Tantra ke paraspar sambandh par alag views.

Shruti (Veda) aur Smriti (Dharmashastra) ko Vaidik pramana mana jaata hai, Purana aur Itihasa unke poorak hain. Agama aur Tantra apni pramanikta khud batate hain. In sab ke rishte par teen mukhya mat milte hain.

  • Tantra Veda ka anuyayi ya poorak hai. Shaiva Siddhanta mein Veda aur Agama ek hi Shiva-gyan ke do roop maane jaate hain, Agama ko vishesh adhikar ke liye.
  • Tantra ek svatantra pramana hai. Kuch Shaiva-Shakta granth khud ko Kali-yuga ya vishesh adhikari ke liye adhik upyukt batate hain.
  • Kuch Vaidik-smarta vidwan tantrik vidhiyon ko avaidik maante rahe aur unse doori banai.
Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye 'Tantra Veda-virodhi hai ya Veda-anuyayi?' ka ek hi jawab nahi hai. Jawab us sampraday aur uske kaal par nirbhar karta hai.

PramanaPadoux, The Hindu Tantric World; Goudriaan aur Gupta (granth-vargikaran).

Tantra ko keval sexuality, black magic ya chamatkar se jodne wali galatfehmiyan.

Tantra ke granth-samuh ka bahut bada hissa darshan (jaise Pratyabhijna), mandir-puja (Agama), mantra aur yoga par hai. Transgressive Kaula vidhiyan ek chhota aur sampraday-vishesh ang hain, aur unki vyakhya bhi baar-baar prateekatmak aur aantarik tareeke se ki gayi hai. Shatkarma wale granth maujood hain, par woh poora Tantra nahi, aur kai acharya unki ninda karte hain. Shaiva Siddhanta aur Vaishnava Pancharatra jaisi dharaon mein transgressive ang prayah nahi milte.

Kyun / Aadhar

'Tantra yaani sex' ya 'Tantra yaani kala-jaadu' wali chhaviyan mukhya roop se aadhunik media aur bazar ne badhayi hain. Isliye is syllabus mein har vishay ko uske asli granthiya anupat mein padhna sikhaya gaya hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh kehna bhi galat hoga ki tantrik granthon mein yeh sab hai hi nahi. Kuch granthon mein vamachara aur abhichara ka varnan hai. Unhe chhupana nahi, sahi anupat aur sandarbh mein samajhna hai (Adhyay 28, Adhyay 29).

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Tantra shabd ka mool arth 'bunai ya dhancha' hai; 'gupt jaadu' wali chhavi baad ki hai.
  • Tantra, Agama, Yamala, Kalpa jaise shabd alag-alag dabbe nahi; arth sampraday aur kaal ke hisaab se badalta hai.
  • Har baat par poochhein: yeh mool granth ki hai, tika ki, guru-vani ki ya modern lekhak ki?
  • Hindu, Buddhist aur Jain Tantra alag paramparayein hain; Neo-Tantra inse alag ek aadhunik cheez hai.
← HomeAdhyay 2 →

PART I · Neev aur parampara

02

Tantra ka itihas

तन्त्र का इतिहास

Tantra kab, kahan aur kaise bana, yeh vidwanon mein bhi puri tarah tay nahi hai. Is adhyay mein saboot, parampara ke daave aur aadhunik anuman ko alag-alag karke dekhna seekhenge.

Basic9 vishay

Prarambhik tantrik paramparaon ki utpatti par scholarly debates.

Kuch vidwan Tantra ki jadein Vaidik aur Upanishadic dharaon mein dhundhte hain. Kuch use yaksha-yogini pujan, matrika-upasana aur lok-dharmik paramparaon se jodte hain. Kuch Pashupata aur Kapalika jaise Shaiva tapasvi sampradayon ko aadhar maante hain. Adhiktar vidwan maante hain ki Tantra kisi ek sthan ya ek samay ki upaj nahi, balki kai dharaon ka milan hai.

Tantrik granthon ka praarambh lagbhag 5vi–7vi sadi ke aaspaas dikhta hai, aur zyadatar granth 8vi sadi ke baad ke hain. Buddhist Tantra lagbhag 7vi sadi se spasht dikhta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is par koi ek nishchit sahmati nahi hai. Isliye 'Tantra Veda se bhi purana hai' ya 'Tantra poori tarah bahari prabhav hai' jaise saaf daave sandehaspad hain.

PramanaFlood aur Sanderson jaise vidwanon ke shodh ka saar; Padoux, The Hindu Tantric World.

Shaiva aur Shakta tantric corpus ka vikas.

Shaiva paramparaon ko aksar do bade hisson mein dekha jaata hai. Pehle tapasvi-pradhan marg hain (Pashupata, Lakula), phir mantra-marg aata hai jisme Shaiva Siddhanta aur Bhairava-Tantra (Trika, Kaula aadi) aate hain. Mantra-marg ke granth hi aage Kashmir ke advaita darshan tak pahunchte hain.

Shakta paramparayein Shaiva dhara ke bheetar aur saath-saath badhin: Kali-kula aur Krama, Tripura (Shri Vidya), Kubjika. Baad mein Bengal mein Kali, Tara aur Dash Mahavidya par kendrit Shakta Tantra ka alag vikas hua.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki Shaiva aur Shakta ek-doosre se kat kar alag nahi, balki ek hi tantrik duniya ki judi hui dharayein hain.

PramanaAlexis Sanderson ke Shaiva Tantra par shodh-patra; Goudriaan aur Gupta.

Madhyakaleen Bharat mein mandir, math, pitha aur acharya-paramparaon ka yogdan.

Madhyakaal mein rajaon ke sanrakshan mein bade mandir bane aur Shaiva acharya raj-guru ke roop mein kaam karte the. Math (aashram-kendra) aur pitha (pavitra sthan) sikhaane aur granth surakshit rakhne ke kendra bane. Acharya-paramparayein shishya-krama ke saath granth aur vidhi ko aage badhati rahin.

Kyun / Aadhar

Tantra sirf ek vyaktigat sadhana nahi tha; woh mandir, raj-darbar aur samaj ke bheetar sangathit roop mein bhi chalta tha. Isi ne granthon ko sadiyon tak bachaya.

PramanaSanderson, The Saiva Age (Shaiva dharm ka rajya-sanrakshan mein vistar).

Kashmir, Bengal, Odisha, Assam, Kerala, Tamil Nadu aur Nepal ke regional developments.

KshetraPehchaanUdaharan
KashmirTrika, Pratyabhijna, Spanda, Krama; Abhinavagupta ka kendraTantraloka, Shiva Sutra
BengalShakta Tantra, Kali aur Tara upasana; baad ke nibandha-granthKrishnananda Agamavagisha ka Brihat Tantrasara
OdishaShakta-Shaiva dhara, Buddhist tantra ke sthal, yogini mandirHirapur aur Ranipur-Jharial ke yogini mandir
AssamKamakhya aur Kamarupa ki Shakta paramparaKalika Purana, Yogini Tantra
KeralaMandir-ritual ke tantrik granth; tantri parivarTantrasamuccaya
Tamil NaduShaiva Siddhanta, Agama-adharit mandir, Tamil tantrik kavyaTirumantiram
NepalKubjika, Newar Buddhism, rajkiya Devi-upasana; purane manuscript surakshitKathmandu ghati ki manuscript-sampada
Dhyan rakhein

Yeh table sirf sankshipt dishanirdesh hai. Har kshetra mein ek se zyada dhara aur kai upparampara thi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kshetra jaan lene se granth ka sandarbh (lipi, bhasha, devata, sampraday) pehchan mein aata hai.

PramanaSanderson; Padoux; Nepal-German Manuscript Preservation Project (manuscript sangrah ke liye).

Tantra ka Tibet, Nepal, China, Japan aur Southeast Asia tak prasaar.

Buddhist Tantra Tibet pahuncha (8vi sadi se aage, aur 11vi sadi mein dobara bade star par). Nepal mein Newar Buddhism ne use jeevit rakha. Tang-kaleen China mein tantrik Buddhism ke anuvad aur shikshak aaye. Japan mein 9vi sadi mein Kukai ne Shingon parampara sthapit ki. Southeast Asia mein Java, Cambodia aur Bali mein Shaiva aur Buddhist tantrik tatva milte hain; Bali ki Hindu paddhatiyon par aaj bhi Shaiva Siddhanta ka prabhav hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Tantra sirf Bharat ka naam nahi; yeh Asia bhar mein anuvad, anukoolan aur nayi rachnaon ke saath phaila.

PramanaSamuel, Tantric Revisionings; Tantra in Practice (White, sampadak); Kukai aur Shingon par standard shodh.

Manuscript copying, textual transmission aur regional recensions.

Purane granth taad-patra, bhoj-patra aur baad mein kagaz par haath se copy kiye gaye. Har naye copy mein galtiyan, sudhaar aur kabhi jaanbujhkar badlav aa sakte the. Nepal ki sookhi hawa ne bahut purane manuscript surakshit rakhe, jabki dakshin mein tazaa copy hote rehne se naye pathantara bane. Alag kshetron ke lipi-roop (Sharada, Newari, Malayalam, Grantha) bhi arth-vyakhya ko prabhavit karte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isiliye ek hi naam ke granth ke do sanskaran alag ho sakte hain. Jab tak aap jaante nahi ki aap kaun-sa recension padh rahe hain, tulna adhoori rahegi.

Dhyan rakhein

Rudrayamala jaise naam se kai alag granth bhi jude hain. Edition ki jaanch zaroori hai (Adhyay 4).

Colonial period mein Tantra ka punarvachan aur Arthur Avalon/John Woodroffe ki bhoomika.

Aupniveshik kaal ke kai European aur missionary lekhak Tantra ko ashlil aur patit batate the. John Woodroffe (Calcutta High Court ke nyayadhish) ne Arthur Avalon naam se is chhavi ke viruddh Tantra ko darshan aur sadhana ke roop mein prastut kiya aur 'Tantrik Texts' ke sampadan karaye. Unhone Bengal ke panditon ke saath milkar kaam kiya.

Julian Strube ka shodh dikhata hai ki Bengal mein is aupniveshik muthbhed ne hi aaj ki 'Tantra' shreni ko bahut had tak banaya.

Kyun / Aadhar

Woodroffe ne Tantra ko padhne yogya aur sammanit viṣay banaya, par unki vyakhya apne samay ke Vedantik aur apologetic drishtikon se rangi hai. Unhe aadhunik shodh aur mool granthon ke saath padhna chahiye.

PramanaWoodroffe (Avalon), Shakti and Shakta, The Serpent Power; Strube, Global Tantra.

20vi–21vi sadi mein academic study, lineage revival aur popular interpretations.

20vi sadi mein Sanderson, Padoux, Dyczkowski, Flood aur White jaise vidwanon ne mool granthon par kathor sampadan aur itihas ka kaam kiya. Gopinath Kaviraj ne Hindi aur Sanskrit mein tantrik sahitya ka parichay diya. Kuch lineages (Shri Vidya ke parivaar, Kashmir Shaivism ki guru-parampara) ne apne granth aur vidhi ko sahejkar naye shishyon tak pahunchaya. Saath hi, 1960–70 ke baad Pashchim mein yoga aur Neo-Tantra ki lokpriya dharayein bani, aur aaj online bazar bhi bahut sakriya hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh teen rasta alag hain: academic shodh, jeevit parampara, aur lokpriya bazar. Padhte samay yeh pehchanna zaroori hai ki aap kis stroat se seekh rahe hain.

PramanaKaviraj, Tantrik Sahitya; Padoux; Sanderson.

Historical evidence, traditional claims aur modern speculation mein antar.

StarKya haiUdaharan
SabootShilalekh, tithi-yukt manuscript, mandir ke avshesh, kisi datable lekhak dwara uddhranAbhinavagupta ka Tantraloka mein purane granthon ko uddhrit karna
Paramparik daavaGranth ya sampraday ka apna kathan'Yeh Shiva ne Parvati ko sunaya', 'yeh anadi hai'
Aadhunik anumanAaj ke lekhak ke daave jinka theek saboot nahi'10,000 saal purani gupt vigyan'
Kyun / Aadhar

Paramparik daave ko 'jhootha' kehna zaroori nahi, par use itihas ka saboot bhi nahi maan sakte. Ek achhi aadat: har baat ke saath poochhein, 'iska aadhar kya hai?'

Dhyan rakhein

Bina saboot ke bade daavon se bachein, khaaskar jab koi 'gupt tantra' ke naam par paisa ya adhikar maange (Adhyay 10).

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Tantra ek jagah ya ek samay ki upaj nahi; yeh kai dharaon ka milan hai.
  • Tantrik granthon ka sakriya kaal lagbhag 6vi–14vi sadi hai, jisme 8vi sadi ke baad adhikansh granth bane.
  • Kshetra, lipi aur recension jaane bina granth ki tulna adhoori rahti hai.
  • Woodroffe jaise lekhakon ko mool aur aadhunik shodh ke saath padhein.
← Adhyay 1Adhyay 3 →

PART I · Neev aur parampara

03

Pramukh dharayein aur sampraday

प्रमुख धाराएँ और सम्प्रदाय

Tantra ek akhand paddhati nahi; yeh kai dharaon ka samooh hai. Is adhyay mein bharat aur bahar ki pramukh tantrik dharaon ka sankshipt parichay aur unke beech ka farq milega.

Basic12 vishay

Shaiva Siddhanta.

Shaiva Siddhanta ek dwaitwadi (dualist) Shaiva dhara hai. Isme teen tatva maane gaye hain: Pati (Shiva), Pashu (jeevatma) aur Pasha (bandhan). Shiva mukti ka kaaran aur anugraha ka data hai. Yeh dhara mandir-puja aur 28 Agama-granthon par aadharit hai aur Veda ke saath ek-sut hone ka daava karti hai.

Tamil Shaiva Siddhanta mein 13vi sadi ke Meykandar ka Sivajnanabodham kendriya granth hai. Uttar Bharat mein Siddhanta ke Sanskrit acharya (jaise Bhatta Ramakantha) alag dhara chalate the.

Kyun / Aadhar

Siddhanta ko samajhna isliye zaroori hai ki ise Kashmir ke advaita Shaivism ke saath aksar mila diya jaata hai, jabki dono ke siddhant alag hain.

PramanaMeykandar, Sivajnanabodham; Shaiva Agama (Kamika, Karana, Kirana aadi).

Non-dual Kashmir Shaivism: Trika, Pratyabhijna, Spanda aur Krama.

Yeh advaita (non-dual) Shaiva dharayein hain jinme chetna (chiti) hi ek-matra satya hai. Trika shabd teen ka vachak hai (Shiva, Shakti aur nara/jeev, ya Para-Parapara-Apara shaktiyan). Pratyabhijna ka arth 'pehchaan' hai: jeev ko apni asli Shiva-svarup ki pehchaan karni hai (Utpaladeva). Spanda chetna ki gatisheel dhadkan ko kehta hai (Vasugupta/Kallata). Krama Kali-upasana ki ek kram-mool dhara hai.

Is dhara ke shikhar acharya Abhinavagupta (10vi–11vi sadi) aur Kshemaraja hain. 'Kashmir Shaivism' naam aadhunik hai; paramparik log in dharaon ko alag-alag naamo se jaante the.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dhara aage ke adhyayon (tattva, mala, upaya, matrika) ka mukhya aadhar hai.

PramanaShiva Sutra; Spanda Karika; Ishvarapratyabhijna Karika; Tantraloka.

Kaula parampara aur uski vibhinna shakhayein.

Kula ka arth 'parivar' ya 'samuh' hai. Kaula dhara mein Shakti ka parivar (yogini aur shaktiyan) aur guru-kula, dono mahatvapurn hain. Kaula kai roop mein milta hai: Trika ke bheetar ka Kaula ang, Bengal aur Assam ka Shakta Kaula, Kubjika-Kaula, aur Nath-Kaula (jaise Matsyendranath se jude Kaulajnananirnaya ki parampara).

Kyun / Aadhar

Kaula ek chhatri-shabd hai; ek granth ko Kaula kehne se uske sampraday-vishesh siddhant ya vidhi ka patta nahi chalta. Iske liye granth ka naam aur sampraday dekhna padta hai.

PramanaKularnava Tantra; Kaulajnananirnaya; Sanderson.

Shakta: Kali-kula, Shri-kula aur anya Devi-centred traditions.

Shakta dhara mein Devi hi param tatva hai. Kali-kula mein Kali (aur Krama jaisi dharayein) mukhya hain, aur yeh aksar bhayanak aur rahasyamay swaroop ke saath judi hai. Shri-kula mein Tripura Sundari (Lalita) mukhya hain aur upasana saumya, saundarya aur gyaan-pradhan roop mein hoti hai (Shri Vidya).

Devi Mahatmya (Markandeya Purana ka ansh), Devi Bhagavata aur Shakta Upanishad is dhara ke Puranic aur Vedantik aadhar hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Shakta ko Kali-pooja tak seemit karna galat hoga. Shri-kula ki parampara ka tone aur anushthan Kali-kula se kaafi alag hai.

PramanaDevi Mahatmya; Lalita Sahasranama; Brooks, Auspicious Wisdom.

Kubjika aur Yogini traditions.

Kubjika dhara ek vistrit Kaula-Shakta parampara hai jiska mool granth Kubjikamatatantra mana jaata hai. Ise aksar Pashchima-amnaya (paschim ki parampara) se joda jaata hai aur Nepal ki Newar paramparaon mein yeh bahut jeevit rahi. Yogini traditions mein yoginiyon (devi-shaktiyon) ko kendra mein rakha gaya. Odisha ke Hirapur aur Ranipur-Jharial jaise gol, chhat-rahit 64-yogini mandir isi parampara ki bhautik nishani hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dharayein aam jan ki jaankari mein kam hain, par academic shodh mein bahut mahatva rakhti hain.

PramanaDyczkowski, The Canon of the Saivagama and the Kubjika Tantras of the Western Kaula Tradition; Kubjikamatatantra.

Nath parampara aur Hatha Yoga.

Nath dhara mein Matsyendranath aur Gorakhnath mukhya acharya hain. Ise 'Navanath' (nau Nath) ki parampara bhi kehte hain. Hatha Yoga ke granth (jaise Hatha Yoga Pradipika, 15vi sadi, Svatmarama) is dhara se nikat se jude hain. Kanphata yogi is parampara ka ek jeevit roop hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Aaj ke yoga ka bahut-sa ang Nath aur tantrik paramparaon se utar kar aaya hai, par aadhunik studio-yoga aur paramparik Hatha Yoga ek jaise nahi hain.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika; Gorakshashataka; White, The Alchemical Body.

Veerashaiva/Lingayat aur Shaiva textual traditions.

Veerashaiva (Lingayat) dhara 12vi sadi mein Basavanna ke netritva mein Karnataka mein ubhri. Ismein har anuyayi apne sharir par ishtalinga dharan karta hai aur sadhana Shatsthala (chhah avasthaon) ke dwara mani jaati hai. Kannada Vachana sahitya is dhara ki bhakti aur samajik samanta ki aawaz hai, aur Veerashaiva Agama ise siddhantik aadhar dete hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Lingayat pehchaan par samudayik bahas hai; kuch log khud ko alag dharm maante hain. Is vishay par bolte samay samman ke saath kaha jaana chahiye.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dhara Shaiva textual traditions ka ek alag, bhakti-pradhan aur jaati-virodhi swaroop dikhati hai.

PramanaBasavanna ke vachana; Veerashaiva Agama.

Vaishnava Pancharatra aur Vaikhanasa.

Pancharatra Vaishnava Agama ki dhara hai. Isme Vishnu ke char vyuha (Vasudeva, Sankarshana, Pradyumna, Aniruddha) aur Lakshmi (Shri) ki Shakti-roop mein upasana milti hai. Parampara se ise 108 Samhita se joda jaata hai. Vaikhanasa dhara Vaikhanasa-sutra aur Taittiriya Vedic ritual se jude Vishnu-pujan ka alag marg hai. Tirumala ka mandir Vaikhanasa paddhati se, aur Srirangam jaisa mandir Pancharatra paddhati se jaana jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Dono Vaishnava hain, par vidhi aur granth alag hain. Isiliye 'Vaishnava Tantra' ek hi chhaap nahi.

PramanaAhirbudhnya Samhita; Lakshmi Tantra; Vaikhanasa Samhita.

Saura aur Ganapatya traditions.

Saura dhara mein Surya param devata hain. Konark, Modhera aur Martand jaise mandir aur Samba Purana jaisi rachnayein is dhara ki hain. Ganapatya dhara mein Ganesha param devata hain. Ganesha Purana, Mudgala Purana aur Ganapati Atharvashirsha iske mukhya granth hain, aur Maharashtra ke Ashtavinayaka sthan iske jeevit roop hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dhara Hindu Tantra ke bheetar chhoti, par alag aur samriddh paramparayein hain, jo aksar Shaiva-Vaishnava-Shakta ke saamne dhyan se chhoot jaati hain.

PramanaSamba Purana; Ganesha Purana; Mudgala Purana.

Buddhist Vajrayana.

Vajrayana Mahayana Buddhism ka tantrik roop hai jo lagbhag 7vi sadi se spasht dikhta hai. Tibetan paramparaon mein tantra ko chaar shreniyon (Kriya, Charya, Yoga, Anuttarayoga) mein baanta jaata hai. Mahasiddha, Nalanda aur Vikramashila jaise vidyalay isme mahatvapurn rahe. Tibet, Nepal aur Japan (Shingon) ne ise surakshit rakha aur aage badhaya.

Kyun / Aadhar

Hindu aur Buddhist Tantra mein mantra, mandala aur deity yoga jaisi samanta dikhti hai, par unke darshan aur lakshya (jaise Buddhatva) alag hain. Isliye tulna sambhal kar karni chahiye.

PramanaGuhyasamaja, Hevajra, Cakrasamvara, Kalachakra tantra; Mahavairocana Tantra; Tattvasamgraha.

Jain mantra, yantra aur ritual traditions.

Jain paramparaon mein mantra (jaise Namokar mantra) aur yantra (jaise Siddhachakra) ka sthan hai. Rakshak devata-roop mein Padmavati (Parshvanatha ki shasan-devi) aur Jvalamalini (Chandraprabha ki) jaisi devi-upasana aur Kshetrapala jaise devata milte hain. Kai 'Kalpa' granth 10vi–11vi sadi mein likhe gaye (jaise Jvalamalini-kalpa aur Bhairava-Padmavati-kalpa).

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh upasana Jain ahimsa aur Tirthankar-bhakti ke dayre mein hoti hai, isliye isko Hindu Tantra ki nakal na samjhein. Aadan-pradan zarur hua, par framework alag hai.

PramanaJvalamalini-kalpa; Bhairava-Padmavati-kalpa; Jain mantra-shastra par shodh.

Kerala temple Tantra, lok-devata aur regional Devi-upasana.

Kerala mein mandir-puja ka mukhya granth Tantrasamuccaya hai (15vi sadi, Chennas Narayanan Namboodiripad) aur uski kai tikayen hain. Tantri parivaron ki parampara se mandiron mein pratishtha aur puja ki jaati hai. Iske saath Bhagavati aur Bhadrakali ki lok-upasana, aur Theyyam jaise anushthan Dravidian lok-paramparaon aur Tantra ke milan ko dikhate hain. Anya rajyon mein Mariamman, Sitala jaisi gram-devi ki upasana bhi isi 'lok' dhara ki hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Tantra sirf pothi mein nahi; yeh aaj bhi mandiron aur lok-utsavon mein jeevit hai.

PramanaTantrasamuccaya; Kerala ke mandir-ritual par shodh.

Dhyan mein rakhein

Yeh sabhi dharayein ek hi Tantra school ke chapter nahi hain. Inka granthiya itihas, ritual paddhati aur dharmik lakshya alag ho sakte hain. Kisi ek ke niyam dusri par laagu mat karein.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Shaiva Siddhanta dwaitwadi hai; Kashmir Shaivism advaita hai. Dono ko mat milayein.
  • Shakta mein Kali-kula (rahasyamay, ugra) aur Shri-kula (saumya, Shri Vidya) do alag swar hain.
  • Vaishnava, Saura, Ganapatya, Buddhist, Jain, aur Kerala/lok paramparayein bhi Tantra ke bade parivaar ka hissa hain.
  • Kisi granth ko Kaula, Shakta ya Tantra kehne se pehle uska sampraday dekhein.
← Adhyay 2Adhyay 4 →

PART I · Neev aur parampara

04

Granth-vigyan aur text-reading methodology

ग्रन्थ-विज्ञान और पाठ-पद्धति

Tantrik granth padhne ke liye sirf Sanskrit aana kaafi nahi; edition, pathantara aur tika ka vivek bhi chahiye. Yeh adhyay granth ko sahi tarah padhne ka hunar sikhata hai.

Madhyam9 vishay

Granth ki pahchan: title, author-attribution, sampraday, recension.

Kisi granth ko padhne se pehle chaar baatein dekhein.

  • Title: Tantra, Agama, Samhita, Karika, Sutra jaise shabd granth ke prakar batate hain.
  • Author-attribution: kai granth Shiva ya Devi ke vachan ke roop mein diye gaye hain (daivya-pratipadan), aur kuch ke manushya lekhak ka naam maloom hai (jaise Abhinavagupta).
  • Sampraday: yeh granth kis dhara ka hai (Siddhanta, Trika, Shri Vidya, Kaula aadi).
  • Recension: granth ke kai paath-roop maujood ho sakte hain, aur sabhi barabar nahi hote.
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh chaar prashn granth ke arth, pramanikta aur seema ka pehla andaza dete hain. Inke bina ek hi naam ke do alag granth ek maan liye jaate hain.

PramanaGoudriaan aur Gupta, Hindu Tantric and Sakta Literature (granth-vargikaran ke liye).

Adhyaya, patala, ullasa, ahnika aur khanda jaise structural terms.

ShabdArthUdaharan
AdhyayaAdhyay, sabse aam shabdKai Purana aur Agama
PatalaParat ya khand; tantrik granthon mein aamKai Shakta Tantra
Ullasa'Ullas' ya khandKularnava (17 ullasa), Mahanirvana (14 ullasa)
Ahnika'Din' ka khand; kramik adhyayTantraloka (37 ahnika)
KhandaBada vibhag ya kandTripura Rahasya ke khanda
Kyun / Aadhar

Hawale (reference) dene ke liye yeh shabd aavashyak hain. 'Tantraloka ahnika 3' jaisa sandarbh tabhi samjha ja sakta hai jab shabd ka arth maloom ho.

PramanaTantraloka (Abhinavagupta), Kularnava Tantra, Mahanirvana Tantra ke sanskaran.

Mool Sanskrit, transliteration, translation aur commentary ka tulnatmak adhyayan.

Ek shloka ko chaar star par padhein: mool Sanskrit, uska Roman ya Devanagari transliteration, ek anuvad, aur ek tika ki vyakhya. Phir dekhein ki anuvad ne mool ke kaun-se shabd chhode ya badle. Har naye technical shabd ko ek glossary-notebook mein mool shabd ke saath likhte jayein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ek hi anuvad par bharosa karne se vyakhya ki galti sidhi aap tak pahunchti hai. Tulna se dikhta hai ki anuvadak ne kahan apna chunav kiya.

PramanaShiva Sutra jaise chhote granth se shuru karna sabse achha abhyas hai.

Pathantara aur ek shloka ki variant readings.

Pathantara ka arth hai ek hi pankti ke alag-alag paath. Yeh kai karanon se banta hai: copy karne ki galti, kshetriya recension, aur maukhik parampara ka antar. Vidwan sanskaran (critical edition) mein har pathantara ko apparatus ke neeche likhte hain, taki padhne wala dekh sake ki kaun-sa paath kis manuscript mein hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kabhi ek akshar ka antar arth badal deta hai, aur mantra mein to uchcharan hi mahatvapurn hai. Isliye pathantara ko pehchanna aavashyak hai.

PramanaCritical editions ke 'apparatus criticus' khand.

Manuscript aur printed edition mein antar; critical edition kya hai.

Manuscript haath se copy kiya gaya prati hai. Purane printed edition aksar ek-do manuscript par bane the aur kabhi sudhaar bhi chupke se kar diye jate the. Critical edition (sameekshatmak sanskaran) mein kai manuscript ki tulna ki jaati hai, sabhi pathantara dikhaye jaate hain aur sabse bharosemand paath tay kiya jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Jab aap ek aisa printed text padhte hain jo critical edition nahi hai, to samjhein ki aap ek vyakti ki chuni hui prati padh rahe hain. Shodh-star ke kaam ke liye critical edition behtar hai jahan ho.

Dhyan rakhein

Bazaar mein purane edition ki naye cover ke saath chhapi hui reprint aksar bina sudhaar ke hoti hai. Sampadak aur prakashak ka naam jaanch lein.

Sanskrit technical terms ka translation karne mein hone wale losses.

Kuch shabd anuvad mein apna pura arth kho dete hain.

  • Chiti ko 'consciousness' kehna uski svatantra, kriyashil shakti ko dhak deta hai.
  • Maya ko 'illusion' kehna bhram paida karta hai; Shaiva darshan mein yeh vastavik avaran-shakti hai.
  • Spanda ko 'vibration' kehna adhura hai; yeh chetna ki dhadkan hai.
  • Tattva ko 'principle' ya 'category' dono kaha jaata hai, par donon pura nahi batate.
Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye achhi aadat hai ki mool shabd ko rakh kar, uske saath anuvad aur chhoti vyakhya likhein.

PramanaPadoux; Wallis, Tantra Illuminated (technical shabdon par).

Tika-parampara ka mahatva; dating, authorship aur historical context.

Tika dikhati hai ki sampraday ke bheetar ek granth ko kaise samjha gaya. Jayaratha ki Viveka Tantraloka ko khol kar samjhati hai; Kshemaraja ki vyakhyaen Spanda aur Svacchanda jaise granthon par hain; Bhaskararaya ki tikayen Shri Vidya ke granthon par.

Lekin tika mool se baad ki hoti hai aur tikakar ka apna drishtikon hota hai. Isliye tika ka kaal aur lekhak bhi jaanch ka vishay hai. Dating ke liye vidwan manuscript ke colophon, shilalekh, aur dusre lekhakon dwara uddhran jaise saboot dekhte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Tika ke bina kai granth aprapya rehte hain, par tika ko mool ke barabar maan lena bhi galat hai.

PramanaTantraloka (Jayaratha ki Viveka ke saath); Nityashodashikarnava (Bhaskararaya ka Setubandha).

Online transcription aur commercial edition verify karna.

Online e-text (jaise Muktabodha ke) haath se type kiye jaate hain aur unme typing ki galtiyan ho sakti hain. Kisi bhi mahatvapurn paath ko mool scan ya vidwan edition se milakar dekhein. Commercial publication ke liye prakashak, sampadak aur ISBN dekhein, aur yeh bhi ki kya woh purani edition ki reprint hai. 'Original', 'pramanik' jaise shabd bechne ke liye aksar likhe jaate hain; unki jaanch aapko khud karni hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Bharosemand sandarbh hi shodh ki neev hai. Ek galat transcription kai logon tak ek hi galti pahuncha sakti hai.

PramanaMuktabodha Digital Library; Institut français de Pondichéry ke digital collections.

Sanskrit padaccheda, anvaya, sandhi aur basic grammar.

Padaccheda ka arth hai jude hue shabdon ko alag karna. Anvaya ka arth hai shabdon ko sahi vyakaran-krama mein rakhna taki vaakya ka arth saaf ho. Sandhi woh niyam hai jisse do shabd milte waqt dhwani badalti hai (jaise ca + eva = caiva).

Ek chhota udaharan lijiye. Pratyabhijnahridayam ka pehla sutra: चितिः स्वतन्त्रा विश्वसिद्धिहेतुः. Padaccheda: chitih | svatantra | vishva-siddhi-hetuh. Arth: chiti (chetna-shakti) svatantra hai aur vishva ki siddhi (nirmaan) ka hetu hai. Anvaya: chitih svatantra (asti), vishva-siddhi-hetuh (asti).

Kyun / Aadhar

Basic vyakaran seekhe bina hamesha anuvad par nirbhar rehna padega. Thodi si vyakaran bhi tika aur mool ke beech ka fark samajhne mein madad karti hai.

PramanaPratyabhijnahridayam, sutra 1 (Kshemaraja); Shiva Sutra 1.1 (chaitanyam atma).

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Granth padhne se pehle title, lekhak, sampraday aur recension dekhein.
  • Ullasa, ahnika, patala jaise shabd hawale dene ke liye zaroori hain.
  • Critical edition kai manuscript ki tulna se banta hai; ise purane printed text par tarjih dein.
  • Technical shabd ko anuvad mein khone se bachane ke liye mool shabd likh kar rakhein.
← Adhyay 3Adhyay 5 →

PART II · Darshan aur tattva-gyan

05

Shiva–Shakti tattva

शिव–शक्ति तत्त्व

Tantrik darshan ki sabse buniyadi jodi hai Shiva aur Shakti. Is adhyay mein dekhenge ki dono kya hain, kaise judte hain, aur is se vishva, moksha aur upasana ke baare mein kya nikalta hai.

Madhyam9 vishay

Shiva aur Shakti ki metaphysical vyakhya.

Kashmir Shaivism ke anusaar Shiva param chetna hain: swayam-prakash, jo kabhi badalti nahi. Shakti unki svatantrya, yaani apni marzi se prakat hone, jaanne aur karne ki shakti hai. Dono do alag cheezein nahi, balki ek hi satya ke do pehlu hain, jaise aag aur uski jalane ki shakti.

Shakta paramparaon mein Devi ko param tatva maana ja sakta hai aur Shiva ko uska ek roop. Isliye yeh jodi alag-alag granthon mein alag anupat se samjhai gayi hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh jodi har agle vishay ka aadhar hai: tattva, mantra, upasana aur yog. Ise galat samjhne par aage ki baatein bhi ulajh jaati hain.

PramanaPratyabhijnahridayam sutra 1; Saundarya Lahari ka pehla shloka (Shiva Shakti se yukt hon tabhi kuch karne mein samarth hain).

Prakasha aur vimarsha.

Prakasha chetna ka swayam-prakash roop hai: woh jo sab kuch prakat hone deta hai. Vimarsha us chetna ka swayam ko jaanne wala pehlu hai, yaani 'main hoon' ka bodh. Prakasha akela ek nirjeev sheeshe jaisa hota; vimarsha use jeevant swa-bodh deta hai. Kashmir Shaivism mein prakasha ko Shiva aur vimarsha ko Shakti se joda jaata hai.

Ek sampradayik upma hai: darpan mein shahar dikhna. Darpan chetna hai, shahar jagat hai, aur jagat darpan se alag nahi, par darpan mein hi dikhta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh shabd-jodi samajhne se aap dekhte hain ki chetna ko sirf 'shant gawah' maanne wali samajh Tantra mein adhoori maani jaati hai.

PramanaAbhinavagupta ke Tantrasara aur Paramarthasara mein darpan ki upma; Ishvarapratyabhijna Karika.

Srishti, sthiti, samhara, tirobhava aur anugraha/panchakritya.

Shiva ke paanch karya (panchakritya) hain: srishti (rachna), sthiti (pehchaan banaye rakhna), samhara (vilay), tirobhava (swayam ko dhak lena) aur anugraha (kripa, yaani apni asli pehchaan wapas dikhana). Nataraja ki murti ko aksar inhi paanch karyon ki chhavi mana jaata hai: damaru se srishti, abhaya-mudra se sthiti, agni se samhara, pair ke neeche Apasmara se tirobhava aur uthe hue pair se anugraha.

Kashmir Shaivism mein yeh paanch ek-ke-baad-ek charan nahi, balki chetna ki sada chalti kriyaein hain. Tirobhava aur anugraha hi bandhan aur mukti ko sambhav banate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vyakhya batati hai ki mukti kisi dusri jagah nahi, usi chetna ke andar ek karya (anugraha) hai.

PramanaShaiva Siddhanta aur Trika granthon mein panchakritya; Nataraja ki iconography par Chidambaram parampara ki vyakhya.

Shakti ke roop aur karya.

ShaktiArthKarya
ChitPrakash, shuddh chetnaSatta ka aadhar
AnandaAtm-sukhChetna ka swaabhavik aanand
IchhaSankalpSrishti ki shuruaat
JnanaJaannaVishva ko prakat karna
KriyaKarnaRachna ki sakriyata

Is panch-shakti suchi ke alawa Trika mein teen devi-shaktiyan (Para, Parapara, Apara) aur Shakta mein Mahakali, Mahalakshmi aur Mahasaraswati jaisi triad bhi milti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Alag-alag sampraday Shakti ko alag tareeke se ginte hain. Yeh sabhi ek hi Shakti ke alag pehlu hain, na ki alag-alag devatayen.

PramanaPratyabhijna aur Trika granth (panch shakti); Devi Mahatmya (triad).

Spanda aur consciousness ki dynamic nature.

Spanda ka arth hai ek sookshm dhadkan ya kampan. Chetna na to sthir patthar hai aur na hi bechain halchal; woh ek lay ke saath khulti (unmesha) aur simatti (nimesha) rehti hai. Spanda Karika ka prarambhik shloka kehta hai ki Shiva ke unmesha aur nimesha se jagat ka udaya aur vilay hota hai.

Is dhara ka aadhar Vasugupta aur Kallata ki Spanda Karika (lagbhag 9vi sadi) hai, aur Kshemaraja ne uspar vyakhya likhi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Spanda ka vichar batata hai ki chetna 'kuch na karne wali' nahi, balki sakriya aur jeevant hai, jo yog aur mantra-sadhana ki dhaarna ko aadhar deta hai.

PramanaSpanda Karika; Kshemaraja ka Spandanirnaya aur Spandasandoha.

Pratyabhijna: asli swaroop ki pehchaan.

Pratyabhijna ka arth hai 'dobara pehchanna'. Is darshan ka mool vichar hai ki moksha koi nayi cheez pana nahi, balki yeh pehchanna hai ki main pehle se hi Shiva-swaroop hoon. Bandhan isliye nahi ki swaroop kho gaya, balki isliye ki us par bhoolne ka parda pada hai.

Utpaladeva ne Ishvarapratyabhijna Karika (lagbhag 10vi sadi) mein is vichar ka tarkik roop diya. Abhinavagupta ne uski Vimarshini tika likhi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse tantrik marg ka lakshya badal jata hai: 'kuch banna' nahi, balki 'jo hoon use pehchanna'. Isi karan is darshan mein anubhavatmak aur tarkik dono tareeke judte hain.

PramanaIshvarapratyabhijna Karika (Utpaladeva); Pratyabhijnahridayam (Kshemaraja).

Parama Shiva, Shakti aur jagat ka sambandh.

Kashmir Shaivism ke anusaar jagat bhram nahi hai. Jagat Shakti ka swayam ko apni chetna ke andar prakat karne ka vastavik roop hai, jise abhasa kehte hain. Parama Shiva (Anuttara, yaani jis se uttam kuch nahi) iske upar-neeche sab mein vyapt hain. Isi karan is mat ko aksar abhasavada kehte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Advaita Vedanta mein aksar jagat ko maya (mithya) maana jaata hai, jabki yahan jagat vastavik prakat roop hai. Yeh antar aage sadhana ke drishtikon ko bhi badal deta hai: jagat ko tyagna nahi, pehchanna.

PramanaTantraloka (Abhinavagupta) ke prarambhik adhyay.

Bheda, bhedabheda aur abheda models.

ModelMool bhavUdaharan
Bheda (dwaita)Ishvar aur jeev alag hainShaiva Siddhanta
BhedabhedaJeev aur Ishvar alag bhi hain, ek bhiKuch Shakta aur Vaishnava vyakhyaein
Abheda (advaita)Ek hi satyaKashmir Shaivism, Advaita Vedanta
Dhyan rakhein

Yeh teen naam sansaar ki sab vyakhyaon ko saaf dabbon mein nahi bandhte. Kashmir Shaivism ko aksar 'abheda' kaha jaata hai, par yahan jagat vastavik prakat roop maana gaya hai, jo Advaita Vedanta se alag hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh labels sirf shuruaati nakshe hain. Asli granth padhne par har sampraday ki apni nuances milti hain.

Jagat, moksha, jivanmukti aur upasana ke darshanik sambandh.

Agar jagat Shakti ki lila hai aur bandhan swaroop ko bhool jana hai, to moksha pehchan ka naam hai aur upasana us pehchan ki taraf ek raasta. Siddhanta mein diksha bandhan ke 'pasha' ko katati hai. Kashmir Shaivism mein mantra, dhyan aur kabhi upaya-rahit pehchan jaise raaste batate hain. Shakta granth (jaise Kularnava) bhoga aur moksha dono ko ek saath sambhav batate hain.

Jivanmukti ka arth hai jeete-ji mukti, jahan vyakti sansarik jeevan ke bheetar swaroop se jura rehta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki darshan aur upasana alag-alag vibhag nahi. Har upasana-paddhati kisi na kisi darshanik maanyata par tiki hai.

PramanaKularnava Tantra; Tantraloka; Shaiva Siddhanta granth.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Shiva (prakasha) aur Shakti (vimarsha, svatantrya) do alag cheezein nahi, ek hi satya ke do pehlu hain.
  • Panchakritya: srishti, sthiti, samhara, tirobhava, anugraha. Tirobhava aur anugraha bandhan aur mukti dono ko sambhav karte hain.
  • Spanda chetna ki jeevant dhadkan hai; Pratyabhijna ka lakshya 'kuch naya pana' nahi, 'swaroop ko pehchanna' hai.
  • Alag-alag sampradayon mein bheda, bhedabheda aur abheda ki alag nuances milti hain; labels ko seedha na lagayein.
← Adhyay 4Adhyay 6 →

PART II · Darshan aur tattva-gyan

06

36 tattva aur tattva-srishti

३६ तत्त्व और तत्त्व-सृष्टि

Kashmir Shaivism aur kai aur Shaiva paramparayen jagat aur jeev ko 36 tattvon mein baant kar samjhati hain. Yahan poori suchi, uske arth aur groupings saaf rup mein diye gaye hain.

Madhyam7 vishay

36 tattvon ki poori suchi (kram aur arth).

Neeche poori suchi hai. Original syllabus mein indriya, tanmatra aur mahabhuta ke naam sirf kram-sankhya ke roop mein diye the (jaise 'Jnanendriya 1'); yahan unke standard naam bhare gaye hain.

KramTattvaDevanagariSaral arth
1ShivaशिवParam chetna, swayam-prakash
2Shaktiशक्तिShiva ki svatantra shakti (vimarsha)
3Sadashivaसदाशिव'Aham idam': 'main hi yeh vishva hoon'; 'main' ka bhav pradhan
4Ishvaraईश्वर'Idam aham': 'yeh vishva main hoon'; 'yeh' ka bhav pradhan
5Shuddhavidyaशुद्धविद्या'Aham aham, idam idam': 'main' aur 'yeh' dono saaf, par dono ek
6MayaमायाBhed ka parda: 'main alag, yeh alag'
7KalaकलाSankuchit kartritva (thoda hi kar sakne ka bhav)
8Vidyaविद्याSankuchit gyaan
9RagaरागAdhoorepan ka bhav, aasakti
10Kala (samay)कालSamay ki seema (bhoot, vartaman, bhavishya)
11NiyatiनियतिSthan aur karan ki seema
12PurushaपुरुषSankuchit anubhav karne wala
13Prakritiप्रकृतिAvyakta prakriti, teen gun ki samya-avastha
14Buddhiबुद्धिNishchay karne wali buddhi
15AhamkaraअहंकारSankuchit 'main' ka bhav
16Manasमनस्Sankalp-vikalp karne wala man
17Shrotraश्रोत्रKaan (jnanendriya)
18Tvakत्वक्Tvacha (jnanendriya)
19Chakshuचक्षुस्Aankh (jnanendriya)
20Jihvaजिह्वाJihva (jnanendriya)
21Ghranaघ्राणNaak (jnanendriya)
22Vakवाक्Vani (karmendriya)
23PaniपाणिHaath (karmendriya)
24PadaपादPair (karmendriya)
25PayuपायुUtsarjan (karmendriya)
26Upasthaउपस्थPrajanan aur aananda (karmendriya)
27Shabdaशब्दDhwani (tanmatra)
28Sparshaस्पर्शSparsh (tanmatra)
29RupaरूपRoop (tanmatra)
30RasaरसSwad (tanmatra)
31Gandhaगन्धGandh (tanmatra)
32AkashaआकाशAakash (mahabhuta)
33VayuवायुVayu (mahabhuta)
34Tejasतेजस्Agni, tej (mahabhuta)
35JalaजलJal (mahabhuta)
36Prithviपृथ्वीPrithvi (mahabhuta)
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh suchi chetna ke 'upar se neeche' utarne ka naksha hai: pehle paanch shuddha star, phir Maya aur uske parde, phir Purusha-Prakriti aur akhir mein sharir aur sansar ke tatva.

PramanaTantraloka (Abhinavagupta); Tantrasara; Shaiva Siddhanta ke tattva-nirupan.

Tattva-srishti: ek-ke-baad-ek kaise prakat hota hai.

Srishti ko upar se neeche ki utran (avarohana) ki tarah padhein. Shiva aur Shakti se Sadashiva mein 'main' ka bhav pradhan hota hai, Ishvara mein 'yeh vishva' ka bhav pradhan hota hai, aur Shuddhavidya mein dono ka santulan aata hai. Phir Maya ke parde se 'main aur yeh alag' ka bhav aata hai, jiske baad paanch kanchuka (kala, vidya, raga, kala, niyati) chetna ko sankuchit karte hain. Is sankuchit chetna ko Purusha kehte hain. Ab Prakriti, buddhi, ahamkara aur manas aate hain, aur uske baad indriyan, tanmatra aur mahabhuta, yaani sharir aur sansar.

Mukti ka raasta isi utran ko ulta karna maana jaata hai: sankoch se wapas vistar ki taraf.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is kram se samajh aata hai ki bandhan kisi bahari shatru se nahi, chetna ke apne sankoch (kanchukas) se aata hai.

PramanaTantraloka ke prarambhik aur madhya ke adhyay.

Shuddha, shuddhashuddha aur ashuddha tattva.

Ek aam (Trika-adharit) vibhajan: pehle paanch tattva (Shiva se Shuddhavidya) shuddha maane jaate hain. Maya, paanch kanchuka aur Purusha (kul saat) shuddha-ashuddha (mishrit) maane jaate hain. Prakriti se Prithvi tak ke 24 tattva ashuddha maane jaate hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Is grouping mein granthon ke beech thoda antar milta hai. Kuch vyakhyaon mein Maya se Prithvi tak sabhi ko ashuddha ginte hain. Jis granth ko padh rahe hain, usi ki grouping dekhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Shuddha tattva mein 'main' aur 'yeh' ka bhed nahi bana hota; ashuddha mein bhed saaf ho chuka hota hai. Isliye yeh banti vaastav mein anubhav ke star ki hai.

Sankhya ke 25 tattvon se tulna.

Sankhya mein 25 tattva hain: Purusha, Prakriti, buddhi, ahamkara, manas, paanch jnanendriya, paanch karmendriya, paanch tanmatra aur paanch mahabhuta. Shaiva 36 tattva mein yahi 25 tattva (kram 12 se 36) maujood hain, aur unke upar 11 aur tattva joda gaye hain: Shiva, Shakti, Sadashiva, Ishvara, Shuddhavidya, Maya aur paanch kanchuka.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki Shaiva darshan ne Sankhya ko radd nahi kiya, balki use ek bade nakshe ke ek hisse ke roop mein apna liya. Sankhya ke 'sabse upar' Purusha-Prakriti ko yahan 'beech ke' tattva maana gaya.

PramanaIsvarakrishna ki Sankhyakarika (25 tattva); Tantraloka (36 tattva).

Prakriti, Purusha, Maya aur kanchuka.

Maya bhed-bhav ka parda hai. Uske baad paanch kanchuka (kavach) chetna ko sankuchit karte hain:

  • Kala: sankuchit kartritva, yaani 'main kuch hi kar sakta hoon'.
  • Vidya: sankuchit gyaan, yaani 'main kuch hi jaanta hoon'.
  • Raga: adhoorepan ka bhav, jisse ichha aur aasakti uthti hai.
  • Kala (samay): samay ki seema.
  • Niyati: sthan aur karan ki seema.

In kanchukon se ghira chetna-bindu Purusha kehlata hai. Prakriti avyakta mool prakriti hai jisme sattva, rajas aur tamas teeno gun samya mein rehte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh paanch kanchuka batate hain ki 'main chhota hoon' ka anubhav ek-ek karke kaise banta hai: thoda kartritva, thoda gyaan, adhoorepan, samay aur sthan.

PramanaTantraloka; Pratyabhijnahridayam (kanchuka ka varnan).

Jiva, pashu, Pati aur bandhana.

Shaiva Siddhanta ki teen shabdavali hai: Pati (Shiva), Pashu (jeev jo bandhan mein hai) aur Pasha (bandhan). Trika mein jeev ko Shiva ka hi sankuchit roop maana gaya hai. Bandhan ke karan teen mala hain, jinhe agle adhyay mein dekhenge.

Kyun / Aadhar

Dono dharayein jeev aur Shiva ke rishte ko alag tarah se dekhti hain, isliye 'pashu' shabd ka arth Siddhanta mein 'sada alag jeev' aur Trika mein 'sankuchit Shiva' ho jata hai.

PramanaMrigendra, Kiranagama (Siddhanta); Tantraloka (Trika).

Tattva ka ritual/diksha mein prayog—sirf sambandhit textual tradition ke context mein.

Kai granth tattva ko diksha ki vyakhya mein bhi lete hain. Shaiva Siddhanta mein diksha ko adhva-shuddhi kaha gaya hai, yaani chhah 'marg' (jinme tattva-adhva bhi hai) ka shodhan. Yeh ek paramparik vishay hai jo us sampraday ke guru aur adhikar ke saath hi samjha jaata hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh sirf tattva ke ritual-sandarbh ka sankshipt parichay hai. Is par koi vidhi yahan nahi di gayi. Diksha ya aise anushthan ko kitab se seekhne ka dava nahi kiya ja sakta.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dikhata hai ki tattva sirf darshan ki baat nahi, kuch sampradayon mein ritual aur initiation se bhi judi hai.

PramanaShaiva Siddhanta ke Agama; Tantraloka (diksha-sambandhit adhyay).

Dhyan mein rakhein

36-tattva model Kashmir Shaivism aur sambandhit paramparaon mein kendriya hai. Shaiva Siddhanta bhi 36 tattva maanta hai, par thode antar ke saath. Vaishnava Pancharatra jaisi anya paramparaon ki apni alag tattva-suchi hai. Isliye har Hindu Tantra par yeh model lagana zaroori nahi.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • 36 tattva = 5 shuddha (Shiva se Shuddhavidya) + Maya aur paanch kanchuka + Purusha-Prakriti + antahkarana + indriya + tanmatra + mahabhuta.
  • Sankhya ke 25 tattva Shaiva 36 mein kram 12 se 36 hain; upar 11 tattva aur jode gaye hain.
  • Paanch kanchuka (kala, vidya, raga, kala-samay, niyati) chetna ko sankuchit karte hain.
  • Shuddha-ashuddha ki grouping granth ke hisaab se thodi badalti hai; apne granth ki list dekhein.
← Adhyay 5Adhyay 7 →

PART II · Darshan aur tattva-gyan

07

Mala, upaya aur mukti

मल, उपाय और मुक्ति

Bandhan kya hai, uska ilaaj kya batate hain Shaiva granth, aur mukti ka matlab kya hai. Is adhyay mein teen mala, chaar upaya aur mukti ke prakar saaf ho jaate hain.

Advanced6 vishay

Anava-mala, Mayiya-mala, Karma-mala.

Teen mala (ashuddhi, yaani bandhan ke karan) maane jaate hain.

  • Anava-mala: mool sankoch, yaani 'main adhoora hoon' ka bhav. Yeh sabse gahra aur pehla mala hai.
  • Mayiya-mala: Maya se utpann bhed-bhav, yaani 'main aur yeh alag hain'.
  • Karma-mala: karmon ki chhaap, jo sukh-dukh aur janm-maran ke chakra se bandhti hai.

Shaiva Siddhanta mein bhi inhi teeno ko 'pasha' mana jaata hai, aur kai vyakhyaon mein Shiva ki tirodhana-shakti (parda dalne wali shakti) ko bhi ek pasha ginte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki bandhan ek nahi, parto mein hai. Upaya aur diksha ki vyakhya bhi isi parat-dar samajh par tiki hai.

PramanaTantraloka (prarambhik adhyay mein mala); Mrigendra aur Kiranagama (Siddhanta).

Pashutva, bandhana aur Shaktipata.

Pashutva ka arth hai bandhe hue jeev ki avastha. Bandhan se mukti tab shuru hoti hai jab Shiva ki shakti jeev par girti hai aur uske andar mukti ki lalak jagati hai. Is ghatna ko Shaktipata kehte hain. Tantraloka mein Shaktipata ki teevrata ke kai star (tivra, madhya, manda aur unke upa-bhed) ginaye gaye hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Aaj kai log 'Shaktipata' ko ek paid transmission ya event ki tarah bechte hain. Granthon mein Shaktipata ko anugraha, yaani Shiva ki kripa, maana gaya hai. Use kharidne ya maangne ka vishay maanna textual aadhar par sahi nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki ichha ya prayas se pehle kripa ka sthan maana gaya hai. Isi karan guru-kripa par itna zor diya jaata hai.

PramanaTantraloka (Shaktipata ka varnan); Kularnava Tantra.

Anavopaya, Shaktopaya, Shambhavopaya, Anupaya.

Kashmir Shaivism chaar upaya (mukti ke raaste) batata hai. Yeh sabse sthool se sabse sookshm ki taraf is kram mein hain:

UpayaKendraSaral arth
AnavopayaKriyaSharir, saans, dharana aur ritual jaise sthool saadhan
ShaktopayaJnanaVichar, mantra aur shuddh-vikalp ka abhyas
ShambhavopayaIchhaSeedhi, tez chetna-jagruti; sankalp ya ek jhalak mein
AnupayaKoi saadhan nahiSeedhi pehchan; 'upaya' ka bhi sahara nahi
Dhyan rakhein

Yahan 'Anava' ka arth 'anu' (vyakti) se hai, jo anava-mala ke 'anava' se alag hai. Anupaya koi 'tareeka' nahi, balki upaya-rahit swa-pehchan ka naam hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vargikaran batata hai ki sabhi sadhak ek jaise nahi hote aur sabhi saadhan ek-barabar nahi. Vijnana Bhairava ki kai dharanayen inhi star mein baatni jaati hain.

PramanaTantraloka (upaya-nirupan); Vijnana Bhairava Tantra.

Guru, anugraha, sadhana aur atma-pehchaan.

Granth guru ko anugraha ka madhyam maante hain: woh vyakti jiske zariye Shiva ki kripa shishya tak pahunchti hai. Sadhana ko 'naya kuch utpann karne wala' nahi maana gaya, kyunki swaroop pehle se maujood hai. Sadhana rukaavaton ko hataane ka kaam karti hai. Phir bhi, Siddhanta mein diksha ek vishesh sanskar hai, jabki Kashmir Shaivism mein guru ki bhoomika samajh aur kripa dono se judi hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki 'sadhana se moksha kharida ja sakta hai' wali dhaarna textual nahi. Anugraha aur prayas ka santulan granthon mein baar-baar aata hai.

PramanaPratyabhijnahridayam; Tantraloka; Kularnava Tantra (guru ka mahatva).

Jivanmukti, videhamukti aur siddhi ka antar.

  • Jivanmukti: jeete-ji mukti, jab vyakti sansarik jeevan ke bheetar swaroop se jura rehta hai.
  • Videhamukti: sharir chhodne ke baad poori mukti.
  • Siddhi: kuch vishesh shaktiyan ya upalabdhiyan (jaise ashta-siddhi); yeh moksha ke barabar nahi.

Kai granth siddhi ko moksha ka rasta nahi, balki ek rukaavat ya pareeksha batate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Siddhi aur moksha ko mila dene se log chamatkar ke peeche bhaagte hain. Granthon mein moksha chetna ki pehchan ka naam hai, na ki shakti ka pradarshan (Adhyay 30).

PramanaTantraloka; Yogasutra (vibhuti-pada); Shaiva Siddhanta granth.

Kashmir Shaivism, Advaita Vedanta aur Shakta advaita ki tulna.

BinduKashmir ShaivismAdvaita VedantaShakta advaita
Param tatvaShiva-Shakti ki ek chetnaNirguna BrahmanDevi, jaise Lalita Tripurasundari
JagatVastavik prakat roop (abhasa)Maya ke karan apparent (mithya)Shakti ka vastavik vilas
Moksha ka raastaPehchan, yog, ritual, kai upayaJnana aur vivekaUpasana aur ritual, jnana ke saath
Moksha ka swaroopShiva-bhav ki pehchanBrahma-swaroop ka bodhDevi se ekatva, kai paramparaon mein bhoga aur moksha dono
Dhyan rakhein

Yeh table sankshipt hai. Har sampraday ke andar kai upa-mat hain, aur 'Tripura Rahasya' jaise granthon mein Shakta aur Vedantik swar mile hue hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Tulna se dikhta hai ki 'sab ek hi hai' kehna bhi galat hai, aur 'bilkul alag hain' kehna bhi. Samanta aur antar dono taakat se maujood hain.

PramanaTantraloka (Advaita Vedanta se antar par adhyay); Tripura Rahasya (Jnana Khanda).

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Teen mala: anava (mool sankoch), mayiya (bhed-bhav), karma (karmon ki chhaap).
  • Shaktipata = Shiva ki kripa ka girna, jo mukti ki lalak jagati hai; use kharidne-bechne ka vishay nahi mana gaya.
  • Chaar upaya: Anava (kriya), Shakta (jnana), Shambhava (ichha), Anupaya (koi saadhan nahi).
  • Jivanmukti, videhamukti aur siddhi alag cheezein hain; siddhi moksha nahi.
← Adhyay 6Adhyay 8 →

PART II · Darshan aur tattva-gyan

08

Vani, dhvani aur matrika

वाणी, ध्वनि और मातृका

Tantra mein shabd sirf bolne ka tool nahi, srishti ka tatva maana gaya hai. Is adhyay mein vani ke chaar star, varnamala aur matrika, aur mantra ke dhwani-vigyan ki neev saaf ki gayi hai.

Madhyam7 vishay

Para, Pashyanti, Madhyama aur Vaikhari.

Vani ke chaar star hain.

  • Para: sabse sookshm, bhed-rahit, chetna se abhinn.
  • Pashyanti: 'dekhne wali' avastha, jahan shabd aur arth abhi ek hain.
  • Madhyama: man ke andar ki vani, vichar ke roop mein.
  • Vaikhari: sunai dene wali, bolchaal ki vani.

Bhartrihari ne Vakyapadiya mein teen star (pashyanti, madhyama, vaikhari) batate the; Tantra ne uske upar Para jodkar chaar star kiye.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dikhata hai ki sunai dene wali awaaz ek bahut gehri parat ka antim roop hai. Mantra-sadhana ki dhaarna isi par tiki hai.

PramanaBhartrihari, Vakyapadiya; Abhinavagupta, Tantraloka aur Paratrishika Vivarana.

Shabda-Brahman aur Vak-shakti.

Shabda-Brahman ka arth hai shabd-roop mein Brahman, yaani vo mool ki dhwani-chetna jisse sab kuch nikalta hai. Bhartrihari ise shabda-tattva kehte hain. Shaiva-Shakta paramparaon mein is shakti ko Vak ya Para Vak kehte hain aur ise param Shakti ke roop mein dekha jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isi dhaarna se mantra ko sirf shabd nahi, devata ka dhwani-roop maana jaata hai.

PramanaBhartrihari, Vakyapadiya; Padoux, Vac: The Concept of the Word in Selected Hindu Tantras.

Sanskrit varnamala, matrika, svara aur vyanjana.

Sanskrit ki varnamala mein 16 svara hote hain (a, aa, i, ee, u, oo, ri, ree, li, lee, e, ai, o, au, anusvara ya am, visarga ya ah) aur 34 vyanjana ('ka' se 'ha' tak ke 33 aur 'ksha' ko milakar 34 ya 35 maane jaate hain). Tantra ke 50 matrika (varn-maatayen) 'a' se 'ksha' tak ginaye jaate hain. Inhe Shakti ke dhwani-roop mana jata hai.

Malini ek alag, mishrit kram hai jo Malinivijayottara aur Tantraloka mein aata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh 50 matrika baad mein chakra ke patton (4, 6, 10, 12, 16, 2, jin ka jod 50 hota hai) aur nyasa mein bhi aate hain (Adhyay 13, Adhyay 21).

PramanaTantraloka (matrika aur Malini par); Shat-chakra-nirupana.

Bindu, nada, visarga aur bija-mantra.

Bindu (anusvara, 'm') ek bindu mein simti hui chetna hai. Nada us dhwani ki goonj hai jo bolne ke baad khamoshi mein ghulti hai. Visarga ('ah') Shakti ke bahar nikalne ya srishti ke roop mein phailne ka sanket hai. Bija-mantra ek chhoti dhwani-ikai hai jo aksar bindu ke saath bolti hai.

Om ko A, U aur M teen matra ke saath aur uske baad ki khamoshi se joda jaata hai, jaisa Mandukya Upanishad mein aata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh teen shabd mantra ki sanrachna samajhne ki kunji hain; inke bina 'hrim' ya 'shrim' ko sirf awaaz maan liya jaata hai.

PramanaMandukya Upanishad; Tantraloka; Vijnana Bhairava Tantra.

Akshara aur consciousness par tantrik interpretations.

Akshara ka arth 'varn' bhi hai aur 'a-kshara', yaani jo kabhi nashta nahi hota, bhi. Tantra mein har varn ko ek shakti-roop mana jaata hai. Kashmir Shaivism ka ek prasiddh vichar hai ki aham ('main') 'a' se 'ha' tak ke saare varno ko aur bindu ko apne mein samet leta hai, yaani 'main' ke andar poora jagat samaya hai. Is poorn 'main' ke bhav ko purnahanta kehte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki Tantra mein bhasha aur chetna alag nahi; 'main' ka sahi bodh hi saare shabdon ka mool hai.

PramanaPratyabhijna granth; Tantraloka; Paratrishika Vivarana.

Mantra, dhyan aur ritual language ka sambandh.

Kai paramparaon mein ek hi devata ke teen roop maane jaate hain: mantra (dhwani-roop), yantra (rekha-roop) aur dhyan ya murti (aakar-roop). Mantra devata ko dhwani se judta hai, dhyana-shloka uska roop man mein banata hai, aur ritual ki bhasha (jaise viniyoga) batati hai ki yeh mantra kis kaam mein lagta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Teeno ko ek saath padhne se dikhta hai ki puja ke alag-alag ang ek hi devata ke alag-alag aayam hain.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Shaiva aur Shakta paddhati-granth.

Uchcharan, svara, chhanda aur matrika-nyasa ka shastriya arth.

Uchcharan ka shastriya adhyayan Shiksha (Vedanga) mein hota hai: dhwani kaun-se sthan se nikalti hai (kantha, talu, murdha, danta, oshtha) aur kaise. Svara Vedic mantra mein pitch (udatta, anudatta, svarita) hota hai; tantrik mantra mein aksar yeh nahi hota, par 'svara' ka arth 'vowel' bhi hota hai. Chhanda matra aur laya ki sanrachna hai (jaise anushtubh). Matrika-nyasa varnon ko sharir ke angon par sthapit karne ki vidhi hai, jiska darshanik arth sharir ko dhwani-roop mananaa hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Mantra ka uchcharan guru se seekhna paramparik taur par mahatvapurn maana gaya hai. Yahan sirf shastriya arth diya gaya hai, vidhi nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Mantra mein ek akshar ka uchcharan badalne se arth ya prabhav badalne ki maanyata hai, isliye uchcharan ko shastra mein itna mahatva diya gaya.

PramanaPaniniya Shiksha; Tantraloka; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Vani ke chaar star: Para, Pashyanti, Madhyama, Vaikhari. Sunai dene wali awaaz sabse sthool star hai.
  • Matrika = 50 varn, Shakti ke dhwani-roop; inhi se chakra ke patte aur nyasa jude hain.
  • Bindu, nada aur visarga mantra ki sanrachna samajhne ke zaroori shabd hain.
  • 'Aham' ('a' se 'ha' tak, bindu ke saath) mein poora jagat samaya hai; yeh Kashmir Shaivism ka ek bada vichar hai.
← Adhyay 7Adhyay 9 →

PART III · Sadhak, guru aur diksha

09

Adhikari aur sadhana ki neev

अधिकारी और साधना की नींव

Kisi bhi tantrik adhyayan ya sadhana se pehle sadhak ki neev kya honi chahiye, yeh adhyay isi par hai: yogyata, naitik anushasan, dincharya, parampara ki pehchan aur un jaalon ki jaankari jahan sadhak aksar phans jaate hain.

Basic8 vishay

Adhikara aur parampara ke anusaar yogyata.

Adhikara ka arth hai kisi cheez ko seekhne ya karne ki yogyata ya haq. Tantra mein yeh do tarah se aata hai: diksha dwara mila adhikara (kis mantra ya kriya ki ijazat) aur sadhak ke swabhav ya taiyari ka adhikara (jaise pashu, vira aur divya bhav). Alag sampraday adhikara ko alag tarah se paribhashit karte hain.

Kai Tantra granth, khaaskar Kaula-Shakta, jaati aur ling ke aadhar par rok ka virodh karte hain. Par vyavhar mein har jagah aisa nahi tha, aur granth aur kaal ke hisaab se bahut antar milta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Adhikara ka sawaal yeh batata hai ki Tantra mein 'sabke liye ek hi tareeka' wali soch nahi hai. Saath hi, 'adhikara' ke naam par kisi ko dabana ya paise maangna uchit nahi.

PramanaKularnava Tantra (shishya ke gun); Shaiva Siddhanta mein diksha-adhikara; Mahanirvana Tantra (grihastha ke liye).

Shraddha, viveka, dhairya aur anushasan.

Shraddha ka arth bharosa ke saath apnapan hai, andhvishwas nahi. Viveka sahi aur galat, asli aur nakli mein fark karne ki samajh hai. Dhairya lambi yatra mein tikne ki shakti hai, aur anushasan niyamit rehne ki aadat hai. Yeh chaaron gun Tantra hi nahi, kai paramparaon mein sadhak ki buniyadi yogyata maane jaate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Viveka ke bina shraddha andhvishwas ban sakti hai, aur shraddha ke bina viveka sirf tark-bahas. Dono saath chahiye. Isi liye guru ko bhi parakhna sikhaya jaata hai (Adhyay 10).

PramanaBhagavad Gita (shraddha par adhyay); Kularnava Tantra.

Ahimsa, satya, shaucha, dayabhava, yama-niyama.

Patanjali ke Yogasutra mein paanch yama (ahimsa, satya, asteya, brahmacharya, aparigraha) aur paanch niyama (shaucha, santosha, tapas, svadhyaya, Ishvara-pranidhana) bataye gaye hain. Tantrik granth bhi sadhana se pehle yehi naitik neev maante hain, aur dayabhav (karuna) ko vishesh mahatva dete hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Naitik anushasan ke bina mantra, yantra ya ritual ko bhi chamatkar ya shakti-lobh ke liye istemal karne ka khatra badh jata hai.

PramanaYogasutra 2.30 aur 2.32; Shaiva aur Shakta granthon mein sadachar ka varnan.

Aahar-vihar, neend, dincharya aur samanya swasthya.

Gita 6.16–17 mein sanyamit aahar-vihar aur sone-jagne ke santulan ko yog ke liye zaroori bataya gaya hai. Hatha Yoga Pradipika ke anusaar zyada khana, atiriktt parishram, bahut bolna, kathor niyamo par ziddi hona, bheed ka sang aur chanchalta yog ko nasht karte hain. Shaktisampann sadhana tabhi sambhav hai jab sharir aur man swasth ho.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isiliye Tantra ke granth bhi sharir ko 'sadhana ka saadhan' maante hain. Bhookha rehna ya ghanton na sona sadhana nahi hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Kisi bhi kathor upvaas, nind kam karne ya anya sharirik prayog se pehle apne swasthya ko dekhein. Bimari ya dawaai ho to doctor ki salah lein.

PramanaBhagavad Gita 6.16–17; Hatha Yoga Pradipika 1.15.

Ishta-devata, kuladevata aur parampara ki pehchan.

Ishta-devata woh devata hain jinhe sadhak apne liye chunta hai ya jo guru dwara diye jaate hain. Kuladevata parivar ya vansh ke devata hain. Sampraday woh jeevit parampara hai (jaise Shaiva Siddhanta, Shri Vidya, Vaishnava aadi) jiske andar sadhana ki paddhati chalti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Apne parivar ya kshetra ki parampara ko jaanna isliye zaroori hai ki sadhana ki jadein wahin hoti hain. Parampara ko jaldbaazi mein chhodkar kuch 'gupt' dhoondhne se bhatkav aa sakta hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth aur kul-parampara; Sharada Tilaka Tantra (ishta-devata ke prasang).

Sankalpa, nitya-upasana, svadhyaya aur diary.

Sankalpa ek aisa dridh nirnay hai jo samay, kriya aur uddeshya ko batata hai. Nitya-upasana roz ki niyamit upasana hai. Svadhyaya khud ki padhai aur aatma-chintan hai. Ek study diary seekhne ka sabse saral upaya hai: roz kya padha, kaun-sa shabd nahi samjha, aur koi sawal.

Kyun / Aadhar

Chhoti par niyamit aadat lambe samay mein badi shuruaat se zyada tikti hai. Diary se aap galat samajh jaldi pakad paate hain.

PramanaYogasutra 2.1 aur 2.44 (svadhyaya); Isi site ka Study roadmap.

Ahamkara, chamatkar-lobh aur siddhi-lobh.

Sadhana mein sabse aam jaal teen hain: apne ko dusron se bada samajhna (ahamkara), kuch chamatkar dekhne ki ichha, aur kisi shakti ki lalach. Yogasutra ki vibhuti-pada batati hai ki siddhiyan samadhi ke raaste mein rukaavat ban sakti hain. Tantrik granth bhi siddhi ko moksha se alag aur kabhi kabhi bhatkaane wala batate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Jab sadhana ka uddeshya 'dikhana' ya 'kuch hasil karna' ho jaye, to woh rukaavat ban jaati hai. Isi liye guru aur samuh ke daavo ko bhi chamatkar ke aadhar par nahi, karm aur vyavhar ke aadhar par parakhna chahiye.

PramanaYogasutra, vibhuti-pada; Tantraloka (siddhi ki seema).

Grihastha-jivan aur sadhana ka sambandh.

Kai tantrik paramparayen grihastha-anukool hain. Mahanirvana Tantra mein grihastha ko hi mukhya adhikari maana gaya hai. Dusri taraf Nath aur sanyasi paramparayen alag raaste par chalti hain. Grihastha ke liye parivar, kaam aur samajik zimmedari pehle aati hai aur sadhana unke saath santulan mein chalti hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Sadhana ke naam par parivar ya aajeevika ko kamzor karna, ya kisi aur ke saath anuchit vyavhar karna kisi bhi parampara ka adarsh nahi hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is se dikhta hai ki Tantra sirf sanyas ya ekant ka raasta nahi, aur sadhana ka ek maapdand hai ki woh jeevan ko sambhalti hai, bigaadti nahi.

PramanaMahanirvana Tantra; Kularnava Tantra.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Adhikara diksha aur swabhav dono se jud sakta hai; alag-alag sampraday ise alag tarah se parakhte hain.
  • Shraddha aur viveka saath chalte hain; ek bina doosre ke adhoora hai.
  • Yama-niyama aur swasthya sadhana ki neev hain, bonus nahi.
  • Ahamkara aur siddhi-lobh sabse aam rukaavatein hain.
← Adhyay 8Adhyay 10 →

PART III · Sadhak, guru aur diksha

10

Guru, parampara aur diksha

गुरु, परम्परा और दीक्षा

Guru, parampara aur diksha ka tantrik jagat mein bahut bada sthan hai. Yeh adhyay unke arth, prakar aur sabse zaroori sawaal par hai: kis par bharosa karein aur kab doori banayein.

Basic8 vishay

Guru, acharya, upadhyaya aur mantra-guru ke roles.

Manusmriti (2.140–141) ke anusaar acharya woh hai jo upanayana karwata hai aur poora Veda sikhata hai, jabki upadhyaya aajeevika ke liye Veda ka ek ansh padhata hai. Tantra mein guru woh hai jo diksha dekar sadhak ko parampara se jodta hai. Mantra-guru wo hai jo mantra deta hai. Shiksha-guru (jo sikhata hai) aur diksha-guru (jo initiate karta hai) alag ho sakte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh bhed samajhne se pata chalta hai ki 'guru' shabd ek hi arth mein nahi aata. Kai sampraday dono ko alag maante hain.

PramanaManusmriti 2.140–141; Kularnava Tantra (guru ke lakshan).

Guru-shishya sambandh, lineage, paddhati aur adhikara.

Guru-shishya sambandh ek parampara-shrinkhla ka hissa hai: guru ke guru, unke guru, aur aage tak. Yeh parampara sampraday ki pehchan ka aadhar hai. Paddhati wo vidhi hai jo is parampara mein sikhayi jaati hai. Adhikara diksha se milta hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Lineage ke daave ki jaanch zaroori hai: kya woh jaanchi ja sakne wali hai, ya sirf naam gina diye gaye hain? 'Meri parampara 5000 saal puraani hai' jaise daave itihas ka saboot nahi hote.

PramanaGuru-parampara suchiyan (granthon ke ant mein); Sanderson (parampara ke itihas par).

Diksha, mantra-diksha, kriya-diksha, abhisheka aur purnabhisheka.

Diksha ka arth hai initiation. Paramparik nirukti ke anusaar yeh woh sanskar hai jo gyaan deta hai aur pap ko kshaya karta hai. Mantra-diksha mein mantra ka prapt hona, kriya-diksha mein kisi ritual ya puja-kriya ka adhikar milna maana jaata hai. Abhisheka ek pavitra jal se kiya jaane wala sanskar hai, aur Shri Vidya jaisi paramparaon mein purnabhisheka ek vishesh uchch-star ka sanskar maana jaata hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh sirf shabdon ka parichay hai. Diksha ke charan, naam aur arth har sampraday mein alag hain, aur unhe seekhna sirf apne guru ke saath hi theek hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is se dikhta hai ki 'diksha' ek hi cheez nahi. Isliye 'mujhe diksha mil gayi' ya 'online diksha' jaise daavon ko parakhne ke liye pehle poochhna chahiye ki kaun-si diksha aur kis parampara ki.

PramanaKamika Agama (diksha ki nirukti); Tantraloka (diksha-vichar); Shri Vidya paddhati-granth.

Shambhavi, Shakti-diksha aur samayadiksha jaise terms—text-specific meanings ke saath.

Alag granth in naamo ko alag arth se prayog karte hain.

  • Kashmir Shaivism mein Shambhavi diksha sabse uchch maani jaati hai, jisme guru ki drishti ya sankalp matra se Shiva ki kripa ka sanchar mana jaata hai.
  • Shakti-diksha Shakta paramparaon mein Shakti ya mantra ke sanchar ke roop mein aati hai.
  • Shaiva Siddhanta mein samaya-diksha pehla charan hai, jisme sadhak ko puja ka adhikar milta hai. Uske baad vishesha aur nirvana diksha aate hain.
  • Shri Vidya mein samayachara ek alag, aantarik marg ke roop mein bhi aata hai, jo Siddhanta ke samaya se alag hai.
Kyun / Aadhar

Ek hi shabd ka arth dusre sampraday mein ulta bhi ho sakta hai. Isiliye shabd-suchi se zyada, granth aur sampraday dekhna zaroori hai.

PramanaTantraloka; Shaiva Siddhanta Agama; Lakshmidhara ki Saundarya Lahari tika.

Guru ke claims ko parakhna.

Kisi guru ya acharya ke daave parakhne ke kuch saral aadhar hain:

  • Kya guru apni parampara aur guru ka naam saaf batate hain, aur kya use aap kisi aur se jaanch sakte hain?
  • Kya woh sawaal poochne dete hain, ya sawaal ko 'shraddha ki kami' kehkar rok dete hain?
  • Kya unke daave ('main chamatkar kar sakta hoon', 'main guaranteed result deta hoon') vyavhaar mein bhi dikhte hain?
  • Kya unka khud ka jeevan sadachar aur anushasan se mel khata hai?
  • Kya paise ya seva ki maang saaf aur seemit hai?
Kyun / Aadhar

Kularnava jaise granth bhi sachche guru ke gun aur nakli guru ki pehchan batate hain. Isliye guru ko parakhna parampara ke virodh mein nahi, parampara ke anusaar hai.

PramanaKularnava Tantra (guru-shishya ke lakshan); Tantraloka.

Financial pressure, fear, exploitation ya isolation ke red flags.

Neeche ke sanket dikhein to savdhaan ho jayein.

  • Paise ki maang jo badhti jaati hai, ya 'upay' ke naam par baar-baar bheti.
  • Darr dikhana, jaise 'bhoot lagega', 'shrap lagega', 'parivar par sankat aayega'.
  • Aapko parivar, mitron ya doosre logon se alag karna.
  • Gopniyata ka istemal sawaal rokne ke liye, jaanch se bachne ke liye.
  • Sharirik ya bhavnatmak dabav, ya 'sirf main bacha sakta hoon' jaisa bhaav.
  • Guaranteed result, jaise 'shaadi 7 din mein', 'paise do guna'.
Dhyan rakhein

Aise lakshan dikhne par doori banayein, kisi bharosemand vyakti se baat karein. Dhokha, jabardasti ya shoshan kanooni dayre mein bhi aa sakta hai; zarurat par police ya pariwar ki madad lein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh sab Tantra ki paramparaon ki seekh nahi, unka durupayog hai. Granth-pramanik Tantra mein bhi darr, paise ya dabav se mukti nahi milti.

PramanaHugh Urban, Tantra: Sex, Secrecy, Politics, and Power (commercial 'secret Tantra' par).
