Independent academic study aur lineage practice ki limits.

Academic study aap ko granth, itihas, bhasha aur aalochana deti hai: yeh sab bina kisi guru ke bhi sambhav hai. Par yeh sadhana ka anubhav ya diksha nahi de sakti. Lineage practice aap ko jeevit parampara, vidhi aur anubhav deti hai, par kabhi kabhi sawal poochne ki jagah kam ho sakti hai.

Ek santulit raasta yeh ho sakta hai: pehle textual samajh banayein, phir yadi sadhana karni ho to apni ruchi ki parampara se adhikar-purvak jodein, aur dono ko ek-doosre ki jaanch ki tarah rakhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Dono akele adhoore hain. Is syllabus mein bhi yahi kaha gaya hai ki independent reading ko automatic authorization na samjhein.

PramanaPadoux; Sanderson; sampraday ke jeevit acharyon ka anubhav.

Multiple gurus par alag sampradayon ke mat.

Alag sampraday ek se zyada guru ko alag tarah se dekhte hain. Kuch paramparaon mein diksha-guru ek hi maana jaata hai, par shiksha-guru kai ho sakte hain. Bhagavata Purana mein Dattatreya ke 24 guru ka prasang batata hai ki seekhna kai jagah se ho sakta hai. Ek prasiddh Sanskrit ukti kehti hai ki shishya ka dhan lene wale guru bahut hain, par shishya ka dukh door karne wala guru durlabh hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ek hi saath kai sampradayon ke guru se mantra lena kuch paramparaon mein bhram ya virodh paida kar sakta hai. Isliye har sampraday ke apne niyam jaanna aavashyak hai.

PramanaBhagavata Purana (11.7–9); guru-vishayak paramparik uktiyan.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Guru, acharya, upadhyaya aur mantra-guru ke arth alag hain; shiksha-guru aur diksha-guru bhi alag ho sakte hain.
  • Diksha ke naam aur arth har sampraday mein alag hain; shabd ki jagah sampraday dekhein.
  • Guru ke daavon ko lineage, vyavhar aur sawaal ki azadi se parakhein.
  • Paise, darr, alag-thalag karna aur guaranteed result bade red flags hain.
← Adhyay 9Adhyay 11 →

PART IV · Mantra-shastra

11

Mantra ka swaroop aur classification

मन्त्र का स्वरूप और वर्गीकरण

Mantra kya hai, kitne tarah ke hote hain aur uske ang kaun-se hain: yeh adhyay mantra-shastra ki buniyadi shabdavali samjhata hai.

Basic8 vishay

Mantra, stotra, sukta, japa aur naam-smarana mein antar.

ShabdMatlabUdaharan
MantraPavitra dhwani-sutra jo devata ya chetna se jodta hai. Paramparik nirukti hai 'mannat trayate', yaani jo manan se raksha karta haiOm namah shivaya
StotraDevata ki prashansa mein rachi kavitaShiva Mahimna Stotra
SuktaVaidik rik-sangrah, ek vishay parPurusha Sukta, Shri Sukta
JapaMantra ko baar-baar dohrane ka abhyasMala par japa
Naam-smaranaDevata ke naam ka yaad rakhna, aksar diksha ke bina bhi'Ram Ram' ya 'Hari bol'
Kyun / Aadhar

In shabdon ko mila dene se log samajh nahi paate ki kis cheez ke liye diksha ya adhikara maana jaata hai aur kis ke liye nahi.

PramanaYaska ki Nirukta aur paramparik tantrik nirukti; Rigveda-Samhita; Bhakti sahitya.

Vaidik aur tantrik mantra ka textual/ritual context.

Vaidik mantra Samhitaon mein milte hain aur unke saath svara (swar-chinha), chhanda aur rishi jude hote hain; unka mukhya prayog yajna mein hai. Tantrik mantra Tantra aur Agama granthon se aate hain, aksar bija-akshar par adharit hote hain aur nyasa aur diksha ke saath chalte hain. Dono ke beech overlap bhi hai: Gayatri Vaidik hai par tantrik puja mein bhi aati hai; Mahamrityunjaya Rigveda (7.59.12) mein hai par Shaiva upasana mein bhi bahut prayog hota hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Pramanikta ke niyam alag hain. Isliye mantra ko 'Vaidik' ya 'tantrik' kehne ke pehle uska srot jaanna zaroori hai.

PramanaRigveda 7.59.12; Gayatri (Rigveda 3.62.10); Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Bija, ekakshara, mantra-pada, mala-mantra aur mantra-mala.

  • Bija: beej-akshar, jaise 'hrim'; ek chhoti, ghani dhwani-ikai.
  • Ekakshara: ek akshar wala mantra.
  • Mantra-pada: kai shabdon wala saamanya mantra.
  • Mala-mantra: bahut lamba mantra, aksar 20 ya adhik akshar ka (ginti granth ke hisaab se alag).
  • Mantra-mala: mantron ki mala ya sangrah, aur kabhi 'varnamala' ke roop mein bhi prayog.
Kyun / Aadhar

Is varg-vibhajan se pata chalta hai ki mantra ki lambai aur sanrachna kaise batayi jaati hai. Sankhya-seemayein granth ke hisaab se badalti hain.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Mantra-mahodadhi (Mahidhara).

Saguna/nirguna upasana aur mantra.

Saguna upasana mein devata roop aur gun ke saath hote hain, aur mantra us roop se jodta hai. Nirguna upasana mein param tatva gun-rahit maana jaata hai, aur mantra (jaise Om, Soham) us ki taraf ishara karte hain. Kai sadhak dono ko mila kar chalate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Gita (12.5) bhi nirguna upasana ko kathin batati hai. Isliye saguna mantra aksar pehle seekhne ka madhyam rahe hain.

PramanaBhagavad Gita 12.2–5; Upanishadic Om-upasana.

Ishta-mantra, guru-mantra aur kulamantra.

Ishta-mantra woh mantra hai jo sadhak ke ishta-devata ka ho. Guru-mantra wo hai jo guru diksha mein deta hai (aksar yahi ishta-mantra hota hai, par hamesha nahi). Kula-mantra parivar ya lineage ka mantra hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Mantra ka sambandh sadhak se hota hai, isliye kai paramparaon mein use bina guru ke chunna ya naya rachna theek nahi maana jaata.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Kaula granth (kula-mantra ka prasang).

Rishi, chhanda, devata, bija, shakti, keelaka, viniyoga.

Kai mantra-paramparaon mein ek mantra ke saath sat ang bataye jaate hain.

AngArth
RishiWoh drashta jisne mantra ko 'dekha'
ChhandaMantra ka chhand (metre)
DevataMantra ke devata
BijaMool beej-akshar
ShaktiMantra ko saamarthya dene wala tatva
Keelaka'Keel' ya sthirta dene wala, jise 'kholna' maana jaata hai
ViniyogaMantra ka uddeshya ya prayog
Dhyan rakhein

Har mantra ke saare ang nahi hote, aur unka prayog parampara ke hisaab se badalta hai. Yeh vahi baat hai jo is syllabus ke 'corrections' mein bhi kahi gayi hai (Zaroori corrections).

Kyun / Aadhar

Stotra aur mantra ke shuru mein jo 'viniyoga' ki ghoshna aati hai (jaise Durga Saptashati ka Kilaka stotra), woh inhi angon ka sanket hai.

PramanaDurga Saptashati ke prarambhik khand; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Mantra ke devotional, meditative aur ritual uses.

Mantra ke teen aam prayog hain: bhakti (devata ka naam-gaan aur prarthana), dhyan (japa ke saath chitta ko ek jagah tikana) aur ritual (puja, homa aur anya vidhiyon mein ang ke roop mein).

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh prayog ek hi mantra ke liye ek jaise nahi hote. Kai mantra ke liye diksha ya vishesh adhikara ka niyam hai, aur kuch sabke liye khule hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is se samajh aata hai ki mantra ke liye 'kya bhi karo, sab chalta hai' ya 'bilkul mat bolo' dono hi galat sarvajanik niyam hain.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Shaiva aur Shakta stotra-sahitya.

Mantra-diksha, adhikara, uchcharan aur pathantara.

Kai paramparaon mein mantra guru se diksha ke saath milna chahiye, taki adhikara, uchcharan aur sahi paath ek saath ho. Internet par kai mantra alag-alag pathon mein milte hain, aur kabhi akshar-bhed ya ashuddhi bhi hoti hai (Adhyay 4).

Dhyan rakhein

Mantra ko online ya kisi anajaan stroat se uthana kuch sampradayon mein theek nahi maana jaata. Is par apni parampara ki salah lein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Pathantara aur uchcharan ka vichar is baat se aata hai ki mantra mein dhwani hi ardh hai.

PramanaTantraloka (uchcharan aur matrika); Paniniya Shiksha.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Mantra, stotra, sukta, japa aur naam-smarana alag hain; adhikara ka sawaal alag-alag.
  • Vaidik mantra svara ke saath yajna mein, tantrik mantra bija aur nyasa ke saath diksha mein jude hote hain.
  • Rishi, chhanda, devata, bija, shakti, keelaka, viniyoga mantra ke ang hain; har mantra ke saare nahi hote.
  • Ishta-mantra, guru-mantra aur kula-mantra alag ho sakte hain.
← Adhyay 10Adhyay 12 →

PART IV · Mantra-shastra

12

Bija, matrika aur mantra ki sanrachna

बीज, मातृका और मन्त्र की संरचना

Bija-akshar, varnamala aur mantra ki bunai samajhne se mantra ek rahasyamay awaaz se badal kar ek saaf-sudhri sanrachna ban jaata hai.

Madhyam5 vishay

Om/Aum, Aim, Hrim, Shrim, Klim aur anya bija-aksharon ki paramparagat vyakhya.

  • Om (pranava): A, U, M teen matra aur bindu ke saath; sabse mool dhwani maani jaati hai.
  • Aim: Saraswati ya Vagbhava-bija, vani aur gyaan se jodi jaati hai; Shri Vidya mein Vagbhava-kuta ka bija.
  • Hrim: Maya ya Hrllekha, Bhuvaneshvari aur Maya-shakti se judi.
  • Shrim: Lakshmi (Shri) ka bija.
  • Klim: Kama-bija; aakarshan aur ichha se jodne ki parampara, kai sampradayon mein Kali ya Krishna se bhi.
  • Anya udaharan: Krim (Kali), Gam (Ganapati), Dum (Durga).
Dhyan rakhein

Yeh sirf paramparagat jodiyan hain. Har bija ke alag sampraday mein alag arth ho sakte hain, aur bija ko 'ek shabd ka anuvad' nahi samjha ja sakta.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is suchi se pata chalta hai ki bija-akshar kis devata-tatva se joda jaata hai, par yeh sampoorn ya antim vyakhya nahi.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Mantra-mahodadhi; Saundarya Lahari tika.

Varnamala, bindu, nada, anusvara aur visarga.

Bija aksar kai varno aur bindu se milkar bante hain. Udaharan ke taur par, hrim ko ha, ra, ee aur bindu se, shrim ko sha, ra, ee aur bindu se, aur klim ko ka, la, ee aur bindu se bana mana jaata hai. Bindu aur nada ke arth ke liye Adhyay 8 dekhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is sanrachna ko samajhne se pata chalta hai ki bija ek akhand dhwani nahi, balki varnon ka sanyojan hai. Yeh matrika ke vichar se judta hai.

PramanaTantraloka; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Mantra mein akshara, pada aur vakya.

Akshara (syllable), pada (shabd) aur vakya (vaakya) mantra ki teen parat hain. Udaharan: Gayatri ka chhand 24 akshar ka hai, jisme teen pada aur har pada mein 8 akshar hote hain. Om namah shivaya mein Om (pranava), namah (namaskar) aur Shivaya (Shiva ke liye) hain, aur ise aksar panchakshari (paanch akshar wala) kehte hain, jab 'Om' ke bina gina jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is tarah ka vishleshan batata hai ki mantra ek vyakaran-yukt rachna bhi hota hai, sirf awaaz nahi.

PramanaRigveda 3.62.10 (Gayatri); Shiva Purana (panchakshara ka prasang).

Mantra, yantra aur dhyana-shloka ka sambandh.

Kai paramparaon mein ek devata ka mantra (dhwani-roop), yantra (rekha-roop) aur dhyana-shloka (aakar ka varnan) saath chalte hain. Puja mein teeno ek saath prayog hote hain: mantra se devata ko bulaya jaata hai, dhyana-shloka se roop mann mein banaya jaata hai, aur yantra pooja ki jagah banti hai (Adhyay 15).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki mantra aur yantra ko alag-alag vastu mana jata hai, par ek hi devata ka saath mein roop.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Shri Vidya paddhati-granth.

Vachika, upanshu, manasika japa; ajapa, Soham/Hamsa aur likhita-japa.

  • Vachika: zor se, sunai dene wala japa.
  • Upanshu: dheere, honth hilte hain par dusre nahi sunte.
  • Manasika: poori tarah man mein.

Manusmriti (2.85) ke anusaar upanshu japa vachika se aur manasika japa upanshu se uttam hai.

  • Ajapa: bina boli hui, saans ke saath chalne wali Hamsa ya Soham dhwani. Kai Nath aur tantrik granth ise din mein hazaron baar swabhavik roop se hota hua batate hain.
  • Likhita-japa: mantra ko likh kar karne wali japa.
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vargikaran batate hain ki japa ek hi tarah ka nahi, aur sthool se sookshm ki taraf jaane ka kram bhi hai.

PramanaManusmriti 2.85; Gorakshashataka; Vijnana Bhairava Tantra.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Bija ke arth sampraday-vishesh hote hain; ek-shabd ka anuvad nahi.
  • Bija aksar varno aur bindu se bante hain; yeh matrika se judta hai.
  • Mantra mein akshara, pada, vakya ki parat hoti hai; chhand aur vyakaran bhi lagte hain.
  • Japa teen tareeke ka: vachika, upanshu, manasika; sookshm roop ajapa (Hamsa/Soham).
← Adhyay 11Adhyay 13 →

PART IV · Mantra-shastra

13

Nyasa aur mantra-kriya ki theory

न्यास और मन्त्र-क्रिया का सिद्धान्त

Nyasa, antaryaga aur mantra-dosha jaise shabd mantra-shastra ki gehri parto mein aate hain. Yeh adhyay unki sirf shabdavali aur darshanik arth batata hai, prayog ki vidhi nahi.

Advanced6 vishay

Kara-nyasa, anga-nyasa, shadanga-nyasa, rishyadi-nyasa, matrika-nyasa.

Nyasa ka arth hai 'rakhna' ya 'sthapit karna', yaani mantra ya devata-bhav ko sharir ke ang par sthapit karna.

  • Kara-nyasa: mantra ko hathon aur ungliyon par sthapit karna.
  • Anga-nyasa: sharir ke angon par sthapit karna.
  • Shadanga-nyasa: chhah angon par: hriday, shir, shikha, kavach, netra aur astra.
  • Rishyadi-nyasa: mantra ke rishi, chhanda aur devata ko kramashah shir, mukh aur hriday par sthapit karne ka sanket.
  • Matrika-nyasa: varno ko sharir ke angon par sthapit karna (Adhyay 8).
Dhyan rakhein

Yahan vidhi nahi, sirf shabdavali aur arth diya gaya hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh shabdavali pata chalne par paddhati-granthon mein aane wale nirdesh samajhna aasan ho jata hai.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Mantra-mahodadhi; Kashmir Shaiva granth (matrika ke prasang mein).

Nyasa ka symbolic/darshanik arth.

Nyasa ka darshanik arth hai ki sadhak ka sharir devata-bhav se bhar jaata hai. Ek prasiddh ukti hai ki devata ban kar hi devata ka poojan karna chahiye. Yeh bhi maana jaata hai ki nyasa se sharir 'mantra-maya' ban jata hai aur sadhak ka sambandh sirf bahar ki murti se nahi, apne andar se bhi hota hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isi dhaarna se pata chalta hai ki Tantra mein sharir ko sadhana ka 'bhoomi' mana gaya hai, ruka-vat nahi.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Abhinavagupta ke granthon mein aantarik yaga ki vyakhya.

Antaryaga aur bahiryaga.

Bahiryaga bahari vastuon (phool, deep, jal aadi) ke saath ki jaane wali puja hai. Antaryaga aantarik, yaani mann mein ki jaane wali puja hai, jisme vastuon ki jagah bhavna aur visualisation hota hai. Kai tantrik granth, jaise Abhinavagupta ke, aantarik puja ko shreshth batate hain, aur kai bahari puja ko uske liye taiyari ya parinaam ke roop mein dekhte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is vibhajan se dikhta hai ki Tantra ka lakshya bahari karmkaand se aage jakar aantarik anubhav tak jaane ka hai.

PramanaTantraloka; Shri Vidya paddhati; Vijnana Bhairava Tantra.

Mantra-dosha, shuddhi, chaitanya aur shapa-vimochana jaise concepts.

Kuch granth mantra ke 'dosha' ginate hain (jaise chhinna, ruddha, shaktihina jaise naam milte hain), aur unki shuddhi ke upay batate hain. Chaitanya ka vichar yeh hai ki mantra ko 'jagrit' karna chahiye, jo kai sampraday diksha ya vidhi se mante hain. Shapa-vimochana ka vichar kuch mantra-prasangon mein milta hai, jaise Durga Saptashati ke saath judne wale paath-nirdesh.

Dhyan rakhein

Aaj kai log inhi sankalpanaon ka istemal darr dikhane ya paise maangne ke liye karte hain. Granth-adhyayan aur bazaar ke daavon ko alag rakhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh sankalpanayen is maanyata se uthi hain ki mantra ka prabhav us ki shuddhata aur 'jagrit' hone par nirbhar karta hai, par is ka koi alag, naapa ja sakne wala saboot nahi hai.

PramanaMantra-mahodadhi; Durga Saptashati ke paath-nirdesh; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Sampuṭ, vidarbha, setu aur anya technical terms—textual study ke roop mein.

Paddhati-granthon mein mantra ko jodne ya ghera karne ke shabd aate hain. Sampuṭ ka arth dibba (dabba-jaisa) hai, yaani ek mantra ko dusre mantra ya bija ke beech mein rakhna. Setu ka arth pul hai, yaani mantra ke saath jodi jaane wali ek vishesh dhwani. Vidarbha, grathana aur anya shabd bhi milte hain, jinki paribhasha granth ke hisaab se badalti hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh sirf shabdavali hai, mantra-prayog ka manual nahi. Inki paribhasha jaanne ke liye mool granth, jaise Mantra-mahodadhi aur Sharada Tilaka, dekhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is shabdavali ko jaan lene se paddhati-granth padhna aasan ho jata hai.

PramanaMantra-mahodadhi; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Japa-sankhya, anushthan, sankalpa aur purashcharana ki theory.

Japa-sankhya ginti hai (aksar 108 manke wali mala par). Anushthan ek nishchit avadhi ya sankhya tak ki niyamit sadhana hai. Sankalpa ek aupcharik nirnay hai jisme uddeshya, samay aur kriya ghoshit hoti hai. Purashcharana mantra ke 'jagran' ke liye ki jaane wali kramabaddh purv-sadhana hai, jisme japa ke saath homa aadi ang jude hain (Adhyay 14). 108 ki sankhya ke kai arth bataye jaate hain, par koi ek sarvamaany nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ginti ka vichar anushasan aur aakar dene ke liye hai. Yeh nahi ki sankhya khud kisi shakti ka srot hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Mantra-mahodadhi.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Nyasa = mantra ya devata-bhav ko sharir ke ang par sthapit karna; kara, anga, shadanga, rishyadi aur matrika iske prakar hain.
  • Nyasa ka darshanik arth: sadhak ka sharir devata-bhav se bharna.
  • Bahiryaga bahar ki puja, antaryaga man ki puja hai.
  • Mantra-dosha aur shapa-vimochana jaise shabdon ke naam par aaj paise aur darr ka vyapar bhi hota hai; inhe granth-adhyayan se alag rakhein.
← Adhyay 12Adhyay 14 →

PART IV · Mantra-shastra

14

Japa, anushthan aur purashcharana

जप, अनुष्ठान और पुरश्चरण

Japa ke prakar, mala ki seva aur purashcharana ki paramparagat theory is adhyay mein saath mein aati hai, mantra-phala ke daavon ki seema ke saath.

Madhyam7 vishay

Nitya, naimittika aur diksha-yukt japa mein antar.

PrakarMatlabUdaharan
NityaRoz ki niyamit japaSubah ya shaam ki sandhya mein
NaimittikaKisi vishesh avsar parGrahan, parv ya tyohar
Diksha-yuktDiksha ke baad, guru ki vidhi ke anusaarGuru-mantra ki japa
Kyun / Aadhar

Is tarah ka bhed batata hai ki japa ek hi cheez nahi; kuch niyamit hoti hai, kuch avsar-vishesh aur kuch adhikara-sapeksh.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Dharmashastra mein nitya aur naimittika karma ka vibhajan.

Mala aur sumeru ke traditional roles.

Mala japa ki ginti ke liye mala hai, jisme aam taur par 108 mani hote hain. Sumeru mala ka sirmauri ya guru-mani hai: japa wahin se shuru aur wahin par khatam mani jaati hai, aur use paar nahi kiya jaata. Mala ke upayog ki vidhi (kis ungli se, kis disha mein) paramparaon mein thodi-thodi alag hoti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Sumeru ka arth ginti ki simarekha banana hai: ek chakra ke baad rukna aur dobara shuru karna. Isse japa niyamit rehti hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Mantra-mahodadhi.

Rudraksha, sphatik, tulsi, kamalgatta aur anya mala materials.

Alag mala-samagri ko alag devata se paramparagat roop se joda jaata hai: Rudraksha Shiva se (aur Rudrakshajabala Upanishad mein iska mahatva aata hai), sphatik (quartz) kuch paramparaon mein Devi ya Lakshmi se, tulsi Vishnu se, aur kamalgatta Lakshmi se. Kuch paramparaon mein haldi ya anya samagri bhi vishesh devata se jodi jaati hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Bazaar mein Rudraksha ki 'mukhi' aur 'durlabh' hone ke naam par bahut daam aur nakli cheezein bechi jaati hain. Mala ki asliyat aur daam ki jaanch karein, aur 'chamatkari' daavon par bharosa mat karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Samagri ka chunaav bhakti aur parampara ka vishay hai, na ki kisi alag shakti ka vyapaar.

PramanaRudrakshajabala Upanishad; Shaiva aur Vaishnava paddhati-granth.

Sadhana-sankalpa aur niyamitata.

Sankalpa ke saath niyamitata ka matlab hai tay samay, tay sthan aur tay ginti par japa karna. Granth niyamitata ko is liye mahatva dete hain ki ek lay banti hai aur man sthir hota hai. Par shuruat mein realistic aur sehat-anukool rakhein: ghar ke kaam, neend aur swasthya ko kam kar ke nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Chhoti par niyamit sadhana lambi, par tuti hui sadhana se zyada tikau hoti hai.

PramanaYogasutra 1.14 (abhyasa ka dridh hona); Paddhati-granth.

Purashcharana ke paramparagat ang: japa, homa, tarpan, marjana aur brahmana-bhojana—jahan source mein diye hon.

Kai paddhati-granth purashcharana ke paanch ang batate hain: japa, homa, tarpana, marjana (abhisheka) aur brahmana-bhojana. Ek aam niyam yeh batata hai ki japa ka dasvan hissa homa, homa ka dasvan hissa tarpana, tarpana ka dasvan hissa marjana, aur marjana ka dasvan hissa brahmana-bhojana kiya jaata hai. Japa ki ginti aksar mantra ke akshar ki sankhya ko ek lakh se gun karke batayi jaati hai (jaise 24 akshar wale Gayatri ke liye 24 lakh).

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh granthon mein batayi gayi theory ka parichay hai, kisi vidhi ka nirdesh nahi. Aisa anushthan bahut kathin aur kharchila ho sakta hai, aur kai paramparaon mein yeh guru ke marg-darshan ke bina nahi mana jaata.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is niyam se dikhta hai ki purashcharana ek poora ritual-paddhati hai, sirf ek ginti nahi.

PramanaMantra-mahodadhi; Sharada Tilaka Tantra; Gayatri-purashcharana ke paramparik niyam.

Sankhya, interruption, prayashchitta aur samapan ke textual rules.

Granthon mein is baat ke niyam milte hain ki ginti kaise rakhi jaye, beech mein rukaavat aaye (jaise bimari ya anya kaaran) to kya kiya jaye, aur anusthan ka samapan kaise ho. Aisi sthiti mein sudhaar ke liye prayashchitta (jaise kuch aur japa ya prarthna) batayi gayi hai. Yeh sab sampraday-vishesh hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh niyam dikhate hain ki sadhana ko 'kisi bhi tarah poora kar do' nahi, balki ek anushasit sanrachna maana gaya. Par shuruaat mein isse dar ya doshbodh ka karan nahi banna chahiye.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Mantra-mahodadhi.

Mantra-phala ke claims aur unki limits.

Tantrik aur stotra granthon mein aksar phalashruti aati hai: 'is ke paath se yeh milta hai'. Yeh ek sahityik aur dharmik parampara hai jo protsahan deti hai. Is par bharosa karna aastha ka vishay hai, kisi naapi ja sakne wali guarantee ka nahi. Mantra se mansik sthirta ya santosh mil sakta hai, par kisi bhi bimari ya aarthik samasya ka ilaaj sirf mantra ko samajhna theek nahi.

Dhyan rakhein

Bimari ho to doctor se, kanooni ya aarthik samasya ho to jaankar vyakti se salah lein. Jo log 'guaranteed result' ya 'paise ke badle mantra' bechte hain, unse savdhan rahein (Adhyay 10).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse pata chalta hai ki phalashruti ko ek tareeke ki prerna ke roop mein padhna chahiye, na ki ek guaranteed vada.

PramanaStotra aur Purana-sahitya ki phalashruti-parampara; Hugh Urban, Tantra (commercial daavon par).

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Japa teen tarah ki: nitya, naimittika aur diksha-yukt.
  • Mala mein sumeru ginti ki seema hai; mala-samagri ke chunaav mein parampara aur bazaar ke daavon ko alag karein.
  • Purashcharana ke paanch ang: japa, homa, tarpana, marjana, brahmana-bhojana; dasvan-hissa niyam granth mein milta hai.
  • Phalashruti ek parampara hai, guarantee nahi; swasthya aur kanooni samasyaon ke liye professional madad lein.
← Adhyay 13Adhyay 15 →

PART V · Yantra, mandala aur sacred geometry

15

Yantra-shastra

यन्त्र-शास्त्र

Yantra ek rekha-chitra hai jo devata ka aadhar mana jaata hai. Is adhyay mein yantra ke ang, mantra aur devata se uska rishta, Shri Chakra ki jhalak aur asli-nakli ki jaanch ke saral sawaal hain.

Madhyam9 vishay

Yantra ka arth aur upasana mein bhoomika.

Yantra ka sidha arth 'yantra' ya 'saadhan' hai. Tantra mein ise ek rekha-roop mein devata ka aadhar ya nivas samjha jaata hai, jisme dhyaan aur puja tikti hai. Mantra devata ki dhwani hai, yantra uski jyamiti-roop sthali, aur murti uska aakar-roop. Yantra se upasak ka dhyan ek bindu ya kendra par tikne mein madad milti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye yantra ko sirf 'sajawat ki cheez' ya 'taveez' samajhna adhoori samajh hai. Granthon mein ise upasana ka ek sakriya ang mana gaya hai.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Shri Vidya paddhati-granth.

Bindu, rekha, trikon, chakra, vritta aur bhupura.

TatvaArth
BinduKendra-bindu; srishti ka mool, devata ka avyakta roop
RekhaSeedhi ya tedhi lakeer; yantra ki sanrachna
TrikonTikon. Neeche ki ore ka tikon aksar Shakti, upar ki ore ka Shiva se joda jaata hai
ChakraKamal-dal ya patti wala ghera
VrittaGol ghera
BhupuraBahar ka chaukor ghera, chaaro disha mein dwar ke saath; devata ke 'nagar' ki seema
Kyun / Aadhar

In chhah shabdon se kisi bhi yantra ki rooprekha padhi ja sakti hai: andar se bahar, bindu se bhupura tak.

PramanaShri Yantra ki sanrachna par Shri Vidya granth; Padoux, The Hindu Tantric World.

Yantra aur mandala ka antar.

Aam taur par yantra ek hi devata ko dikhane wala ek rekha-roop hai, aur Hindu tantra mein zyada pracheelit hai. Mandala ek 'mahal' ya sthali ki tarah hota hai jisme mukhya devata ke saath unka parivar bhi rehta hai, aur Buddhist Vajrayana mein ise manushya-roop devataon ke saath banaya jaata hai. Mandala rang-chitra ke roop mein asthayi bhi ho sakta hai, aur yantra aksar dhatu par khuda hota hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh bhed ek sakht rekha nahi hai. Shri Chakra ko ek hi saath yantra aur mandala dono kaha ja sakta hai, kyunki uske avarana mein kai devi-devta rehte hain (Adhyay 16).

Kyun / Aadhar

Dono shabd kai baar ek-doosre ke badle prayog hote hain, isliye prasang dekhna zaroori hai.

PramanaPadoux; Buddhist Vajrayana ke mandala-granth.

Yantra, mantra aur devata ka sambandh.

Har yantra ka ek devata aur ek mantra se sambandh mana jaata hai. Udaharan ke liye, Shri Yantra Lalita Tripurasundari ka roop hai aur uski upasana Shri Vidya ke mantron ke saath hoti hai (Adhyay 27). Granthon mein ise is tarah kaha jaata hai ki yantra devata ka sharir hai aur mantra uski dhwani.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki yantra akela nahi chalta; use mantra aur dhyana ke saath padhna chahiye.

PramanaShri Vidya granth; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Shri Yantra/Shri Chakra; Kali, Tara, Bagalamukhi, Ganapati aur anya yantra-traditions.

Shri Yantra (Shri Chakra) mein nau tikon aur kai ghere hote hain, aur ise Lalita Tripurasundari ka roop maana jaata hai. Dash Mahavidyaon, jaise Kali, Tara aur Bagalamukhi, ke apne yantra hain. Ganapati, Sudarshana (Vishnu ka chakra), Mrityunjaya aur navagraha ke bhi yantra granthon mein aate hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Yahan koi banane ya poojan ki vidhi nahi di gayi hai. Yantra-lekhan aur sthapana paramparik roop se guru aur adhikara se jude hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh suchi dikhati hai ki yantra-parampara ek hi devata tak seemit nahi, kai sampradayon tak phaili hui hai.

PramanaShri Vidya granth; Dash Mahavidya par Kinsley, Tantric Visions of the Divine Feminine.

Textual diagrams, regional variants aur commercial designs ka antar.

Granth yantra ko banane ke tareeke (yantroddhara) batate hain, aur kshetra ke hisaab se roop-bhed milte hain. Shri Yantra ke hi kai roop hote hain, jaise Bhu-prastara (sapat) aur Meru-prastara (ubhra, pyramid). Bazaar mein bikne wale 'siddh yantra' aksar printed poster hote hain jinme rekhayen ya ginti galat bhi ho sakti hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ek achhi jaanch yeh hai ki kisi yantra ki rooprekha kisi granth se mil rahi hai ya nahi. Shri Yantra mein 4 upar-ki-or aur 5 neeche-ki-or tikon, 43 chhote tikon, aur 8 aur 16 patti wale do kamal-vritta hone chahiye.

PramanaShri Yantra ki sanrachna par Shri Vidya granth; Shri Chakra ke 43 tikon wala varnan.

Bhojpatra, tamra aur anya materials ka historical/ritual context.

Bhojpatra (bhurj ke ped ki chhaal) purane samay mein granth aur yantra likhne ka ek aam madhyam tha. Tamra (taanba) ek sthayi dhatu hai jo kai paramparaon mein yantra utkirna ke liye prayog hota hai. Kuch jagah chaandi, sone ya sphatik (crystal) ka bhi prayog hota hai. Likhne ke liye 'ashtagandha' jaisi sugandhit syahi ka bhi zikr aata hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Samagri ka chunaav sampraday aur granth ke hisaab se badalta hai. Kisi ek dhatu ya samagri ko hi 'asli' kehne wale daavon par savdhaan rahein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh samagri-itihas batata hai ki yantra ka bhautik roop paramparik tha, par uski shakti ka daava dhatu ki keemat se nahi jodna chahiye.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Bhojpatra par pandulipi-shodh.

Sthapana aur pranapratistha ke concepts.

Sthapana ka arth yantra ya murti ko sthapit karna hai. Pranapratishtha ka arth hai us mein devata ki upasthiti ya 'prana' ko mantron se pratishthit karna. Agama mandiron mein yeh ek vistrit vidhi hai, aur ghar ke star par sankshipt roop mein bhi hoti hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Aaj 'pranapratishtha kiya hua yantra' ya 'energised' murti ke naam par bahut bechna hota hai. Is vidhi ko sirf sampraday ke adhikari guru ya pujari se hi jaanna theek hai. Online order ya paise ke badle 'siddh' karne ke daavon par bharosa na karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vichar is maanyata par tika hai ki murti ya yantra mein devata ka 'aavahan' kiya ja sakta hai. Is maanyata ko sampraday ke bhitar hi samjha jaata hai.

PramanaAgama granth (Kamika, Karana aadi) ka pratishtha-khand; Tantrasamuccaya.

Authenticity aur fabricated charts ki verification.

Yantra ya chart ki jaanch ke teen sawaal poochhein:

  • Kya iska srot koi naam ka granth ya paddhati hai?
  • Kya uski rooprekha usi granth ke sanskaran se milti hai?
  • Kya bechne wala 'guaranteed' phal, jaise dhan, vashikaran ya shaadi, ka vada karta hai?

Agar teeno ka jawab saaf nahi, to use bina saboot ke daavon ki tarah dekhein.

Dhyan rakhein

Kuch 'yantra' mein sirf sankhya-chakra (magic square) hote hain aur unhe Tantra ke naam par bechne ka koi granth-aadhar nahi hota. Bade daam par ya darr dikha kar bechne par savdhaan rahein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh jaanch is syllabus ke hi 'authenticity aur verification' ke uddeshya ko poora karti hai.

PramanaHugh Urban, Tantra; Shri Vidya granth.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Yantra devata ka aadhar-roop hai; mantra uski dhwani aur dhyana uska aakar.
  • Shri Yantra mein 9 tikon (4 upar, 5 neeche), 43 chhote tikon, 8 aur 16 patti ke do kamal aur bhupura hota hai.
  • Yantra aur mandala ek jaise nahi, par kai jagah mil jaate hain.
  • 'Siddh' ya 'energised' yantra ke daavon ko saboot se jaanchein.
← Adhyay 14Adhyay 16 →

PART V · Yantra, mandala aur sacred geometry

16

Mandala aur avarana-system

मण्डल और आवरण-व्यवस्था

Mandala devata aur unke parivar ki ek yojana-bandh sthali hai. Avarana-system us parivar ko ghere-dar-ghere kaise baantata hai, yeh adhyay Shri Chakra ke udaharan ke saath samjhata hai.

Advanced7 vishay

Mandala: cosmic order, deity arrangement aur ritual space.

Mandala ka arth 'gol' ya 'chakra' hai, aur Tantra mein yeh kai kaam karta hai: yeh brahmand ka naksha hai, devata aur unke parivar ki vyavastha hai, aur puja ya diksha ki sthali bhi. Hindu paramparaon mein Agama aur homa ke liye alag mandala aate hain, jaise sarvatobhadra. Buddhist Vajrayana mein mandala ek 'mahal' ki tarah banaya jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Mandala ko sirf 'sunder chitra' maanna uske asli karya ko chhod dena hai: woh ek sthan hai jahan devata aur sadhak mil kar ek vyavastha mein aate hain.

PramanaPadoux, The Hindu Tantric World; Tantraloka; Buddhist mandala-granth.

Geometric aur symbolic components.

Mandala ke kuch aam ang aur unka sanket:

AngSanket
Chaukor (bhupura)Prithvi, sthiratha; chaaron disha mein dwar
Gol (vritta)Aakash, chakriya samay
Kamal-dalSrishti ka khulna, pavitrata
TikonShakti aur Shiva ke prateek
BinduKendra, mool, srishti ka udgam
Dhyan rakhein

Har sampraday ka apna arth hota hai. Yahan sirf aam sanket diye hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ghere-dar-ghere banawat se dikhta hai ki mandala 'bahar se andar' ya 'andar se bahar' ek yatra ka naksha bhi hai.

PramanaShri Vidya granth; Buddhist mandala vyakhyaen.

Directional deity arrangements.

Mandala mein devata aksar disha ke hisaab se baithaye jaate hain. Aath dikpala paramparik roop se Indra (poorv), Agni (agneya), Yama (dakshin), Nirriti (nairritya), Varuna (pashchim), Vayu (vayavya), Kubera (uttar) aur Ishana (ishana) maane jaate hain. Brahma ko upar aur Ananta (ya Vishnu) ko neeche mana jaata hai, aur kul das disha gini jaati hain. Buddhist Vajradhatu mandala mein kendra mein Vairochana aur chaaron dishaon mein Akshobhya, Ratnasambhava, Amitabha aur Amoghasiddhi hote hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is vyavastha se dikhta hai ki mandala sirf rekha nahi, ek brahmand-vinyas hai jisme har devata ka sthan nishchit hai.

PramanaVastushastra aur Agama ke mandala-nirdesh; Tattvasamgraha (Buddhist Vajradhatu).

Avarana aur devata-vinyasa.

Avarana ka arth 'avaran' ya 'ghera' hai. Mukhya devata ke charon or kai ghere hote hain, aur har ghere mein devi-devata ya shaktiyon ka ek varg rehta hai. Devata-vinyasa un devata ko unki jagah par sthapit karne ki yojana hai. Shri Chakra mein nau avarana hote hain, aur har avarana ki apni yogini-varg ki pehchan batayi jaati hai (jaise pehle avarana ki Prakata yogini).

Kyun / Aadhar

Avarana-vichar se dikhta hai ki ek devata ke saath poori ek duniya jodi hui hai, aur sadhak us duniya mein andar ki taraf jaata hai.

PramanaNityashodashikarnava; Yoginihridaya; Bhaskararaya ki tikayen.

Shri Chakra ke navavarana ka conceptual study.

Shri Chakra ke nau avarana, bahar se andar ki taraf:

KramAvaranaSanrachna
1Trailokya-mohanaBhupura, chaukor, chaar dwar
2Sarvashaparipuraka16 patti ka kamal
3Sarvasankshobhana8 patti ka kamal
4Sarvasaubhagyadayaka14 chhote tikon
5Sarvarthasadhaka10 chhote tikon
6Sarvarakshakara10 chhote tikon
7Sarvarogahara8 chhote tikon
8Sarvasiddhiprada1 kendriya tikon
9SarvanandamayaBindu
Kyun / Aadhar

Is kram ko samajhne se Shri Chakra ek rooprekha se badal kar ek yatra-naksha ban jaata hai: bahari sansar se kendra ke ananda tak.

Dhyan rakhein

Yahan sirf sanrachna ka parichay hai. Puja ka kram (andar-se-bahar ya bahar-se-andar) aur anya vidhiyan sampraday ke hisaab se alag hain aur guru se hi seekhi jaati hain.

PramanaNityashodashikarnava; Yoginihridaya; Bhaskararaya ka Setubandha.

Mandala, diksha, mantra, nyasa aur dhyana ka sambandh.

Kai paramparaon mein diksha mandala ke saamne hoti hai: mandala diksha ka rangmanch hai. Mantra mandala ke devata ko bulate hain, nyasa sadhak ke sharir ko mandala ke rup mein dhaalta hai, aur dhyan mandala ko man mein dekhta hai. Is tarah sharir bhi ek mandala ban jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is sambandh se samajh aata hai ki mandala, mantra, nyasa aur dhyana ek hi tantrik prakriya ke alag-alag hisse hain.

PramanaTantraloka; Shaiva Siddhanta aur Shri Vidya paddhati-granth.

Tantraloka aur Buddhist Vajrayana mandalas ka comparative study.

Tantraloka mein mandala-vidhi ka vistrit varnan aata hai, jisme ek trishula-kamal (trishulabja) mandala bhi hai. Buddhist Vajrayana mein Vajradhatu aur Garbhadhatu (Shingon) aur Kalachakra jaise bahut vistrit mandala hain. Samanta yeh hai ki dono mein devata-kendrit, sanrachit mandala aur diksha mein unka sthan hai.

Antar bhi bade hain: devata-suchi, darshanik lakshya (Shiva-bhav ki pehchan ya Buddhatva), aur ritual ki bhasha alag hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki 'mandala' kisi ek dharm ki khaas cheez nahi, par har dhara ne ise apne darshan ke hisaab se dhaala.

PramanaTantraloka; Tattvasamgraha; Kalachakra Tantra.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Mandala devata aur parivar ka sanrachit sthan hai, kewal chitra nahi.
  • Avarana = ghera; Shri Chakra mein nau avarana hote hain.
  • Mandala, diksha, mantra, nyasa aur dhyana ek hi prakriya ke hisse hain.
  • Hindu aur Buddhist mandala mein rooprekha ki samanta ke bawajood darshan aur lakshya alag hain.
← Adhyay 15Adhyay 17 →

PART VI · Puja, ritual aur mandir

17

Puja ki basic se advanced theory

पूजा की मूल से उन्नत विवेचना

Puja ke prakar, upachara ki ginti, saamagri ka sanket aur samapan tak ke charan yahan saral rup mein samjhaye gaye hain. Yeh vidhi nahi, siddhant ka parichay hai.

Basic9 vishay

Nitya, naimittika aur kamya puja.

  • Nitya: roz ki niyamit puja.
  • Naimittika: kisi vishesh avsar par, jaise tyohar ya grahan.
  • Kamya: kisi ichha ya uddeshya ko pane ke liye.
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh teen shreniyan Dharmashastra aur Agama dono mein milti hain, aur batati hain ki puja ka uddeshya alag-alag ho sakta hai.

PramanaDharmashastra; Shaiva aur Vaishnava Agama.

Bahir-puja aur manasik puja.

Bahir-puja bahari vastuon se hoti hai. Manasik puja poori tarah man mein hoti hai, jisme vastuon ki jagah bhavna aur kalpana hoti hai. Shankaracharya se jodi Shiva Manasa Puja ek prasiddh udaharan hai. Kai paramparaon mein manasik puja ko bahari puja ke saath ya uske badle bhi sambhav maana gaya hai (Adhyay 13).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki puja ka mool bhav man se judta hai, aur samagri sirf uska ek sadhan hai.

PramanaShiva Manasa Puja (Adi Shankara se joda jaata hai); Tantraloka.

Panchopachara, dashopachara, shodashopachara, rajopachara.

Upachara puja mein devata ko diye jaane wale sewa-roop hain.

NaamGintiAam suchi
Panchopachara5Gandha, pushpa, dhupa, dipa, naivedya
Dashopachara10Panchopachara mein padya, arghya, achamaniya, madhuparka aadi jodna
Shodashopachara16Avahana, asana, padya, arghya, achamana, snana, vastra, yajnopavita ya abharana, gandha, pushpa, dhupa, dipa, naivedya, tambula, pradakshina ya namaskara, visarjana
RajopacharaBahut zyada (kai paddhatiyon mein 64 tak)Chhatra, chamara, darpana, nritya, vadya jaise rajasi sevayen
Dhyan rakhein

Yeh suchiyan granth aur paddhati ke hisaab se thodi badalti hain. Yahan sabse aam roop diya gaya hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ginti se dikhta hai ki puja ko devata ka 'atithi-satkar' ya 'raja-seva' maana gaya. Samarthya ke hisaab se kam ya zyada upachara kiye ja sakte hain.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Purusha Sukta ke 16 mantra se joda jaane wala shodashopachara parampara.

Achamana, sankalpa, asana-shuddhi aur puja-sthana.

Puja shuru karne se pehle kuch taiyari ke charan batayi jaati hain. Achamana jal ke teen ghoont lekar shuddhi ka sanket hai. Sankalpa uddeshya ka dridh nirnay hai. Asana-shuddhi baithne ki jagah ki shuddhi hai. Puja-sthana saaf, shant aur nishchit jagah hoti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh taiyari man aur sthan dono ko ek kendra par laati hai. Iska uddeshya aachaar ka bhay nahi, ekagrata hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Dharmashastra (sandhya ke achamana).

Ghanta, deepa, dhupa, gandha, pushpa, arghya, naivedya ka symbolism.

Ek prasiddh mapping paanch upachara ko paanch tatva se jodti hai: gandha (chandan) prithvi se, pushpa aakash se, dhupa vayu se, dipa agni se aur naivedya jal ya amrita se.

  • Ghanta (ghanti): kai puja-granthon mein kaha gaya hai ki devata ke aagaman ke liye bajayi jaati hai.
  • Dipa: prakash aur agyaan ka nash.
  • Dhupa: sugandh aur shuddhi.
  • Arghya: sammanpurvak diya gaya jal.
  • Naivedya: khaadya ki bhent, jise prasad ke roop mein vaapis liya jaata hai.
Kyun / Aadhar

Prateek-arth batate hain ki har vastu sirf 'rasam' nahi, ek bhav ka sanket bhi hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Agama ke upachara-prakaran.

Avahana, sthapana, visarjana.

Avahana devata ko bulane ka bhav hai, sthapana unhe sthapit karne ka, aur visarjana puja ke baad unhe vida karne ka. Visarjana ka arth devata ka apmaan ya tyag nahi, balki unki upasthiti ka shalinata se samapan hai. Aksar ek kshama-prarthana bhi jodi jaati hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh teen shabd batate hain ki puja ka ek shuru, ek madhya aur ek ant hota hai, jisme upasak devata ke saath rishte ko samman se nibhata hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth.

Dhyan, stotra, kavacha, sahasranama, aarti.

  • Dhyana-shloka: devata ke roop ka man mein chitran.
  • Stotra: devata ki prashansa.
  • Kavacha: suraksha ka stotra, 'kavach' ya dhaal ki tarah.
  • Sahasranama: devata ke hazaar naam (jaise Vishnu ya Lalita sahasranama).
  • Aarti: diye ya kapoor ko ghumakar ki jaane wali ant ki seva.
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh sab ang puja ko bhav, kavya aur sangeet ke saath poora karte hain.

PramanaLalita Sahasranama; Vishnu Sahasranama; Paddhati-granth.

Nirmalya, prasad, samapan.

Nirmalya devata ko arpit ki hui purani vastuen (phool, mala aadi) hain, jinhe shaleenta se sambhala ya vidhi se visarjit kiya jaata hai. Prasad arpit bhojan hai, jo kripa ke roop mein vaapis milta hai. Samapan mein pradakshina, namaskara aur kshama-prarthana ke saath puja ka ant hota hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Arpit vastu ka vyavhaar bhi puja ka ek hissa hai: devata ko diya hua kuch kachra nahi, shalinata ka vishay hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Agama mein prasad aur nirmalya ke niyam.

Ishta, kuladevata aur mandir-devata ki seva mein antar.

Ghar mein ishta ya kuladevata ki puja personal aur parivarik hoti hai, aur lachila bhi. Mandir mein devata ki seva Agama ya sthaniya paddhati se, niyamit kal-vibhajan ke saath, adhikari pujari karte hain. Kuladevata ki puja aksar varsh mein ek ya do baar parivar ke vishesh avsar par hoti hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Ghar ki puja mein mandir ki poori vidhi ki nakal ya mandir mein ghar ka ritual thopne ki koshish na karein; dono ke apne niyam hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is bhed ko samajhna isliye zaroori hai ki 'sahi' vidhi ka sawaal sandarbh se tay hota hai.

PramanaAgama (mandir-puja); kul-paramparaon ki paddhati.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Puja teen prakar ki: nitya, naimittika, kamya; bahir aur manasik dono ko jaanein.
  • Upachara 5, 10, 16 ya adhik ho sakte hain; ginti granth ke hisaab se thodi badalti hai.
  • Avahana se visarjana tak puja ek poora chakra hai; arpit vastu ka vyavhaar bhi us ka hissa hai.
  • Ghar ki puja aur mandir ki puja ke niyam alag hain.
← Adhyay 16Adhyay 18 →

PART VI · Puja, ritual aur mandir

18

Advanced puja concepts

उन्नत पूजा-अवधारणाएँ

Bhutashuddhi, pranapratishtha, homa aur avarana-puja jaise vishay puja ke gahre star par aate hain. Yahan unka arth aur sandarbh diya gaya hai, vidhi nahi.

Advanced7 vishay

Bhutashuddhi, pranapratistha, digbandhana.

Bhutashuddhi ka arth hai sharir ke paanch bhuta (tatva) ki shuddhi. Paramparik vichar mein sadhak apne sharir ko man mein uske karan mein vileen karke devata-yogya sharir ke roop mein phir se gadta hai. Pranapratishtha devata ki upasthiti ko sthapit karna hai. Digbandhana disha-bandhan hai: ek pratikatmak suraksha-ghera jisse puja shuddh rahe.

Dhyan rakhein

Yahan sirf shabdon ka arth diya hai, vidhi nahi. Is tarah ki visualisation-vidhiyan kai logon ko chintit ya asthir bana sakti hain; inhe guru ke bina na karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

In teen vidhiyon se samajh aata hai ki Tantra mein puja sirf bahari kaam nahi, balki sadhak ke apne roop ko badalne ki prakriya bhi hai.

PramanaSharada Tilaka Tantra; Shri Vidya aur Shaiva paddhati-granth.

Kalasha-sthapana, arghya-patra, rishyadi-nyasa aur shadanga-nyasa.

Kalasha jal se bhara pavitra ghada hai, jise puja ke shuru mein sthapit kiya jaata hai aur jisme devata aur tirthon ka aavahan maana jaata hai. Arghya-patra arghya ke jal ka paatra hai. Rishyadi-nyasa aur shadanga-nyasa ke arth ke liye Adhyay 13 dekhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kalasha ka vichar batata hai ki puja ki shuruaat ek 'sthira aadhar' se hoti hai, jo baad mein sabhi devata ke aavahan ka madhyam banta hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Agama ke kalasha-prakaran.

Antaryaga, manasik puja aur avarana-puja.

Antaryaga aur manasik puja ke liye Adhyay 13 aur Adhyay 17 dekhein. Avarana-puja mukhya devata ke saath unke avarana (ghere) ke parivar-devata ki poojan hai, jaise Shri Chakra mein nau avarana ki puja (Adhyay 16). Yeh puja kai paramparaon mein sabse vistrit aur adhikara-sapeksh maani jaati hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki ek hi devata ki puja chhoti, madhyam aur vistrit star par ho sakti hai, aur har star ke apne adhikara hain.

PramanaNityotsava; Parashurama Kalpasutra; Bhaskararaya.

Devata-krama, parivara-devata aur upachara.

Devata-krama devata ki puja ka kram hai: kai paramparaon mein sabse pehle Ganesha aur guru ka smaran hota hai, phir mukhya devata aur phir parivara-devata. Parivara-devata mukhya devata ke saath unke parivar ya sahayak devata hote hain (jaise Shiva ke saath Nandi aur Ganesha). Upachara har devata ke saath unki prakriti ke hisaab se badalte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Kram aur parivar ka vichar puja ko ek vyavasthit samajik sanrachna deta hai, jisme mukhya devata ke saath sab ki jagah tay hoti hai.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Agama.

Homa: agni, kund, samidha, ahuti, dravya aur mantra.

Homa agni mein ahuti dene ka anushthan hai. Agni ko devataon tak arpan pahunchane wala maana jaata hai. Kunda agni ka paatra hai (kai aakar: chaukor, gol, ardhachandra, tikon aadi). Samidha khaas lakdi ki chhoti shakhayen hain. Ahuti arpan hai, jise aksar svaha ke saath diya jaata hai. Dravya arpan ki vastuen hain (ghee, til, anaaj aadi).

Mundaka Upanishad (1.2.4) agni ki saat jihvayon ke naam ginati hai: Kali, Karali, Manojava, Sulohita, Sudhumravarna, Sphulingini aur Vishvaruchi.

Dhyan rakhein

Ghar par bina anubhav ke homa na karein; aag, dhuan aur ghee se jalne ka khatra hota hai. Dhuan se saans ki dikkat ho to ghar ke andar na karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Homa Vaidik aur tantrik dono paramparaon ka mukhya ritual hai, par uska prayog sampraday-vishesh hai.

PramanaMundaka Upanishad 1.2.4; Shrauta aur Grihya sutra; Paddhati-granth.

Tarpana, marjana, prayashchitta aur ritual completion ka textual context.

Tarpana jal se devata (ya pitaron) ko tript karne ka arpan hai. Marjana jal ka chhidakna hai (kai paramparaon mein purashcharana ka ek ang). Prayashchitta puja mein hui chook ke sudhaar ke liye ki jaane wali kriya ya prarthana hai; aksar kshamapana ke shlokon se. Ritual ka ant purnahuti, dakshina aur visarjana ke saath hota hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vyavastha is soch par tiki hai ki puja mein chook ho sakti hai, aur uska ek shaleen samadhan bhi hona chahiye.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Purashcharana ke granth (Adhyay 14).

Puja-paddhati-granth aur mool Tantra ka antar.

Mool Tantra mein siddhant aur ritual ke tukde bikhre milte hain. Paddhati-granth un tukdon ko krama se jodkar, 'ab kya karein' ke roop mein likhte hain. Udaharan: Shri Vidya mein Vamakeshvara Tantra aur Nityashodashikarnava mool hain, jabki Nityotsava aur Parashurama Kalpasutra ritual-krama ke granth hain. Kerala mein Tantrasamuccaya ek aisa hi sanklit granth hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye ek hi puja ki mool aur paddhati mein thodi alag batein milti hain. Dono ko mila kar padhna sabse achha tareeka hai.

PramanaNityotsava; Parashurama Kalpasutra; Tantrasamuccaya.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Bhutashuddhi, pranapratishtha aur digbandhana puja ko sharir aur sthan ki shuddhi se jodte hain.
  • Avarana-puja puja ka vistrit roop hai, jo Shri Chakra jaise yantra mein dikhta hai.
  • Homa mein agni, kunda, samidha, ahuti aur dravya ke saath safety bhi ek zaroori ang hai.
  • Paddhati-granth mool Tantra ke ritual tukdon ko kram mein jodte hain.
← Adhyay 17Adhyay 19 →

PART VI · Puja, ritual aur mandir

19

Mandir-Agama aur murti-shastra

मन्दिर-आगम और मूर्ति-शास्त्र

Mandir ki puja ek vyavasthit parampara hai jiska aadhar Agama granthon mein hai. Is adhyay mein Shaiva aur Vaishnava Agama, murti ke lakshan, mandir ki banawat aur pujariyon ki paramparaon ka saral parichay hai.

Madhyam8 vishay

Shaiva Agama aur mandir ritual rules.

Shaiva Siddhanta ke Agama aam taur par chaar bhagon mein baante jaate hain: Vidya (jnana), Kriya, Charya aur Yoga. Kriya aur Charya mein mandir ke nirman, pratishtha aur nitya-puja ke niyam aate hain. Kai Shaiva mandiron mein din bhar mein kai (aksar chhah) kaal ki puja ki vyavastha rehti hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dikhta hai ki Shaiva Agama sirf darshan ke granth nahi, mandir ke prashasan aur puja ka bhi vistrit niyam-granth hain.

PramanaKamika Agama; Karana Agama; Ishanashivagurudevapaddhati.

Pancharatra aur Vaikhanasa temple worship.

Pancharatra Agama mein Vishnu ke vyuha-roop, murti-pratishtha aur nitya-puja ki vidhi di gayi hai. Vaikhanasa paramparaon mein Vikhanas rishi se jude Vishnu-puja ke apne granth aur vidhi hain. Tirumala ka mandir Vaikhanasa paddhati se aur Srirangam jaisa mandir Pancharatra paddhati se jaana jaata hai (Adhyay 3).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye 'Vaishnava mandir ki puja' ek jaisi nahi; mandir ka Agama pehchanna zaroori hai.

PramanaAhirbudhnya Samhita; Vaikhanasa Samhita.

Daily, periodic aur annual worship.

Mandir mein puja teen star par chalti hai.

  • Nitya: daily kai kaal ki puja.
  • Naimittika: maasik ya parv ke din ki vishesh puja, jaise pradosha ya ekadashi.
  • Varshik utsava: saal bhar ke bade utsava, jaise brahmotsava.

Kai mandiron mein lagbhag har 12 saal mein kumbhabhisheka (mahasnana ya punar-pratishtha) hota hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is kal-vibhajan se mandir ek jeevit sanstha ki tarah chalta hai, jiska apna kalendar aur adhikari varg hota hai.

PramanaAgama granth; Mandir-utsava par shodh.

Murti-lakshana, iconography, mudra, ayudha, vahana aur asana.

Murti ke lakshan Agama, Shilpashastra aur Vishnudharmottara Purana jaise granthon mein diye gaye hain. Inme mudra (hast-mudra jaise abhaya, varada), ayudha (astra, jaise trishula, chakra), vahana (jaise Shiva ka Nandi) aur asana (baithne ki mudra) ka varnan hota hai. Murti ke maap ke liye tala jaisi pranali hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Iconography ka har ang kisi darshanik ya katha-sambandh ka sanket hota hai. Isliye iconography ko 'sirf kala' nahi, 'theology' ki bhasha bhi samjha jaata hai.

PramanaVishnudharmottara Purana; Brihat Samhita; Mayamata aur Manasara (Shilpashastra).

Pratishtha, garbhagriha, mandala aur temple architecture.

Pratishtha mandir mein murti ko sthapit karne ka sanskar hai. Garbhagriha mandir ka sabse bheetari 'garbh' kaksh hai jahan mukhya murti hoti hai. Mandir ka naksha aksar Vastupurusha-mandala par adharit hota hai (64 ya 81 khanon ka grid). Mandir ko devata ya brahmand-purusha ke sharir ke roop mein bhi dekha jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is se dikhta hai ki mandir ki banawat bhi ek darshanik yojana hai, jo mandala aur sharir ke vichar se judi hai (Adhyay 16).

PramanaMayamata; Manasara; Agama ke pratishtha-prakaran.

Dhvaja, bali, utsava aur parivara-devata.

Dhvaja-stambha mandir ka dhwaj-stambh hai; dhwaja chadhna utsava ki shuruat batata hai. Bali-pitha parivar aur rakshak devata ko naivedya dene ka sthan hai. Utsava-murti wo murti hai jo utsava mein pradakshina ke liye nikalti hai. Parivara-devata mukhya garbhagriha ke aas-paas ke chhote mandir ya sthan ke devata hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh ang mandir ko ek sthir dharmik sthal se ek jeevant, utsava-yukt samudaya ka kendra banate hain.

PramanaAgama; Mandir-utsava par shodh.

Agamic priesthood aur temple-service traditions.

Mandiron mein pujari-parampara kai tarah ki hai: kuch kuloon mein pushtainni, kuch mein prashikshit. Tamil Nadu ke Shaiva mandiron mein Adishaiva archak, Kerala mein tantri parivar aur anya kshetron mein Pancharatra ya Vaikhanasa archak jaane jaate hain. Mandir seva ke liye endowment aur devasthanam jaisi prashasanik vyavasthayen bhi hoti hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Mandir ki puja sirf 'vidhi' nahi, ek samajik aur aarthik vyavastha bhi hai. Isliye aksar uske adhikar aur niyam par bahas hoti hai.

PramanaMandir-parampara par academic shodh; Tantrasamuccaya (Kerala).

Living tradition versus printed handbook ka antar.

Kisi mandir ki asli puja uski sthaniya paramparaon, sthala-purana aur guru-shishya paramparaon se chalti hai. Chhapi hui Agama-paddhati ek margdarshak ho sakti hai, par mandir mein vastavik prayog usse alag bhi ho sakta hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Kisi mandir ke ritual ko 'galat' kehne se pehle uski sthaniya parampara jaanein. Bahar se pustak lekar mandir ke pujari ko sudharne ki koshish aksar uchit nahi hoti.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is adhyay ka antim sabak yeh hai ki kitab aur jeevan dono se seekhna zaroori hai.

PramanaMandir-parampara par shodh; Agama aur paddhati-granth.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Shaiva Agama ke chaar bhaag: Vidya, Kriya, Charya, Yoga; mandir ke niyam Kriya aur Charya mein hain.
  • Pancharatra aur Vaikhanasa ki mandir-puja alag hain; mandir ka Agama pehchanein.
  • Murti ke lakshan (mudra, ayudha, vahana, asana) theology ki bhasha hain.
  • Kitab aur mandir ki jeevit parampara dono se seekhein; ek ko dusre par thopna sahi nahi.
← Adhyay 18Adhyay 20 →

PART VII · Sharir, yoga aur antar-sadhana

20

Tantrik sharir-vigyan

तान्त्रिक शरीर-विज्ञान

Tantra sharir ko sirf mans-asthi nahi, kai parto wala ek nakshaa maanta hai. Yeh adhyay us paramparik 'sookshm sharir' ke models ko saaf shabdon mein rakhta hai, aur yaad dilata hai ki yeh adhunik anatomy ka seedha naksha nahi hain.

Madhyam7 vishay

Sthula, sukshma aur karana sharir ke models.

Teen sharir ka vichar kai Vedantik aur tantrik paramparaon mein milta hai.

SharirArthAam samajh
SthulaSthool, dikhne wala sharirMans, asthi, indriya
SukshmaSookshm sharir, jo mrityu ke baad bhi chalta mana jaata haiMan, buddhi, prana, vasana
KaranaKaran-sharir, beej-roopAvidya aur sanskar ka mool
Kyun / Aadhar

Is vibhajan se samajh aata hai ki sadhana sirf bahari sharir par nahi, bheetar ki parto par bhi sochi jaati hai. Alag sampraday in parto ki ginti aur naam alag tareeke se dete hain.

PramanaVedanta ke prakriya-granth; Shaiva aur Shakta granthon mein sookshm sharir ke prasang; Flood, The Tantric Body.

Panchakosha, panchaprana aur upaprana.

Taittiriya Upanishad (Brahmananda-valli) paanch kosha (parat) batati hai: annamaya (bhojan se bana sharir), pranamaya (prana-parat), manomaya (man), vijnanamaya (buddhi) aur anandamaya.

Paanch prana ke kaam paramparik roop se is tarah bataye jaate hain: prana (sans lena, hriday ke kshetra mein), apana (utsarjan, neeche ki gati), samana (pachan, santulan), udana (upar ki gati, vani) aur vyana (poore sharir mein vyapt). Paanch upaprana hain: naga, kurma, krikara, devadatta aur dhananjaya.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh prakriya paramparik vyakhya hai. Adhunik sharir-vigyan ke saath inka seedha mel sthapit nahi hua hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vargikaran hatha-yoga aur pranayama ki vyakhya samajhne ke liye zaroori hai, kyunki wahan bhi inhi shabdon ka prayog hota hai.

PramanaTaittiriya Upanishad (Brahmananda-valli); Hatha Yoga Pradipika; Gheranda Samhita.

Ida, Pingala, Sushumna aur nadi-concepts.

Nadi ka arth hai naali ya pravah-marg. Paramparik yogic vyakhya mein teen mukhya nadi ginayi jaati hain: ida (baayein side, chandra-roop, thandi), pingala (daayein side, surya-roop, garam) aur sushumna (kendra mein, reedh ke saath-saath). Upanishadic aur yogic granthon mein nadiyon ki sankhya 72,000 batayi jaati hai (kuch granthon mein anya sankhya bhi).

Dhyan rakhein

Sankhya 72,000 ek paramparik varnan hai, naapa gaya tathya nahi. Alag-alag granth alag-alag sankhya dete hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Nadi ka vichar sadhana ke 'marg' ko samjhane ke liye banaya gaya model hai, jisme sushumna madhya-marg hai jisse prana-shakti ka urdhva-gaman maana jaata hai.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika (2.1–5 aur 4.x par); Shiva Samhita; Shat-chakra-nirupana.

Chakra, padma, kundalini aur Shakti.

Chakra (chakra) ya padma (kamal) sushumna ke saath sthit 'kendron' ke roop mein varnit hain. Kundalini (kundal, yaani kundali mari hui) ko sharir ke adhar mein soyi hui Shakti ke roop mein samjhaya jaata hai. Uske jaagne ko aur sushumna se uthkar uparale kendron se hokar sahasrara mein Shiva se milne ko, yog ka ek kendriya roopak (metaphor) maana jaata hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Kundalini ek paramparik darshanik aur sadhana-sambandhi vichar hai. Ise sharirik 'urja' ke roop mein kisi naapne wale tathya ke taur par pesh karna sahi nahi. Is par practice ki salah guru aur swasthya-vishesagya dono se lein (Adhyay 22).

Kyun / Aadhar

Kundalini ke vichar mein Shakti ka vichar jura hua hai (Adhyay 5): wahi chetna-shakti sharir mein 'soyi hui' maani gayi hai.

PramanaShat-chakra-nirupana (Purnananda); Avalon, The Serpent Power; Flood, The Tantric Body.

Bindu, nada, prana aur consciousness.

Yogic aur tantrik vichar mein bindu (bindu-roop sankuchit tatva) aur nada (sookshm dhwani) sookshm tatva maane jaate hain, aur prana in dono ko sharir mein chalayi rakhne wali shakti hai. Hatha Yoga mein bindu ko aksar virya-roop mana gaya hai aur uski raksha par zor diya gaya hai, jabki Kashmir Shaivism mein bindu ek bhasha-chetna ka symbol hai (Adhyay 8).

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh shabd alag-alag paramparaon mein alag arth rakhte hain, isliye ek hi shabd ka arth ek jagah se dusri jagah par seedha na lagayein.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika; Nada-bindu Upanishad; Tantraloka.

Granthi, bandhana aur subtle body.

Teen granthi (gaanth) batayi jaati hain: Brahma-granthi (muladhara ke aas-paas), Vishnu-granthi (hriday ke aas-paas) aur Rudra-granthi (ajna ke aas-paas), jinhe bhedna sadhana ki pragati ka roopak maana gaya hai. Bandha teen mukhya hain: mula-bandha, uddiyana-bandha aur jalandhara-bandha, jinka vistrit varnan Hatha Yoga Pradipika (adhyay 3) mein milta hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Bandha aur pranayama sharirik roop se asar karte hain (blood pressure, saans, chakkar). Yeh sirf sandarbh ke liye hai, abhyas ke liye nahi (Adhyay 22).

Kyun / Aadhar

Granthi-vichar sadhana ki pragati ko 'rukaavat ko paar karna' ke roop mein dikhata hai.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika 3; Shiva Samhita.

Nyasa aur mantra se sharir ki symbolic mapping.

Nyasa mein sharir ke ang (jaise hriday, shir, hath) mantra-akshar ya devata se jode jaate hain (Adhyay 13). Isi tarah chakra ke patton par varna (matrika) bataye jaate hain (Adhyay 8, Adhyay 21). Is tarah sharir ek aisa 'naksha' ban jaata hai jisme dhwani, devata aur sthan sab ek saath padhe jaate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dikhata hai ki Tantra mein sharir, bhasha aur devata alag-alag nahi, ek hi yojana ke teen aayam hain.

PramanaTantraloka; Shat-chakra-nirupana; Sharada Tilaka Tantra.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Sthula, sukshma, karana sharir aur panchakosha sookshm sharir ke paramparik models hain.
  • Ida, Pingala, Sushumna aur 72,000 nadi paramparik varnan hain, naapi gayi sankhya nahi.
  • Kundalini ek darshanik aur sadhana-sambandhi vichar hai; use sharirik tathya ki tarah na samjhein.
  • Alag-alag granth alag chakra, nadi aur bindu ka model dete hain.
← Adhyay 19Adhyay 21 →

PART VII · Sharir, yoga aur antar-sadhana

21

Chakra-system study map

चक्र-प्रणाली अध्ययन मानचित्र

Saat chakra ki aaj ki lokpriya list ek lambi parampara ka ek roop hai. Yeh adhyay Shat-chakra-nirupana ke aadhar par ek study-map deta hai aur batata hai ki kaun-si baatein granth mein hain aur kaun-si baad ke jod hain.

Madhyam5 vishay

Chakra-system ka srot aur kitne chakra.

Lokpriya saat-chakra model ka mukhya srot Shat-chakra-nirupana hai, jo Purnananda Yati ke bade granth Shri-tattva-chintamani ka ek adhyay hai (lagbhag 1577 ee.). Ismein chhah chakra aur sahasrara ka varnan hai, yaani paanch se zyada aur saat se kam ka naksha hai, jo aksar 'saat chakra' ke naam se prasiddh hota hai.

Dhyan rakhein

'Saat chakra' sabhi granthon ka universal naksha nahi. Kai Kaula aur Shaiva granthon mein alag chakra aur kendra batayi jaati hain, jaise nau chakra ya solah adhar.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is nirdesh se pata chalta hai ki 'saat chakra, saat rang, saat bija' wali lokpriya sarani kai sthanon par aadhunik sanklan hai, jo granth mein is roop mein nahi milta.

PramanaShat-chakra-nirupana; Avalon, The Serpent Power; Flood, The Tantric Body.

Saat chakra: sankshipt study-table.

Yeh table Shat-chakra-nirupana ke varnan ka sar hai. Rang aur anya kai vivaran alag-alag sampradayon mein alag milte hain.

ChakraSthan (paramparik)Dal (patti)TatvaBijaDevata (aur Shakti)
MuladharaReedh ke adhar4PrithviLamBrahma (Dakini)
SvadhishthanaLing ke mool ke paas6JalVamVishnu (Rakini)
ManipuraNabhi ke paas10AgniRamRudra (Lakini)
AnahataHriday12VayuYamIshvara (Kakini)
VishuddhaKantha16AakashHamSadashiva (Shakini)
AjnaBhrumadhya2Man ka kendraOm (pranava)Shambhu (Hakini)
SahasraraShir ke upar1000Param tatva se juran/aParam Shiva
Dhyan rakhein

Devata aur Shakti ke naam is table mein Shat-chakra-nirupana ke anusar hain; anya granthon mein inka mel nahi milta.

Kyun / Aadhar

Dal ki sankhya 50 matrika se mel khati hai (4+6+10+12+16+2 = 50), yaani chakra ke patton par sansarik varna baithe hain (Adhyay 8).

PramanaShat-chakra-nirupana; Avalon, The Serpent Power.

Har chakra ke liye kya record karein.

Har chakra ke liye ek study-card banayein, jisme ye khane hon:

  • Sanskrit naam aur uska arth.
  • Granth mein sthan (kahan likha hai).
  • Padma ya dal ki sankhya, aur un par kaun-se varna.
  • Bija aur tatva, source ke anusaar.
  • Devata aur Shakti ka naam.
  • Pratikatmak aakritiyan (jaise yantra ki ya jantu ki).
  • Rang aur tatva ka source: kya yeh mool granth mein hai ya baad ka jod?
  • Dhyana se sambandh: granth mein is chakra ka dhyana kis roop mein bataya gaya hai.
  • Dusre granthon ke alag naksha.
  • Aaj ki lokpriya vyakhya aur mool granth mein antar.
Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh card aapko 'chakra ka yeh rang hai' jaisi baat ka source dhoondhna sikhata hai.

PramanaSyllabus ka mool nirdesh; Shat-chakra-nirupana.

Alternate maps aur popular modern interpretations se antar.

Kai paramparaon mein chakra alag-alag ginti mein milte hain: Kubjika granth mein alag chakra aur shaktiyon ke samuh batate hain. Hatha Yoga ke granth shat-kriya aur nadi ko zyada mahatva dete hain. Aaj ki lokpriya sarani, jisme har chakra ka ek rang (indradhanush jaise), ek 'mansik gun' aur ek 'bimari' jodi jaati hai, zyadatar 20vi sadi mein Pashchim mein bani. Is mein Charles Leadbeater ki rachnayon (The Chakras, 1927) ka bhi bada hissa mana jaata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh antar jaan lene se aap shuddh paramparik naksha aur aaj ke wellness-vyavsay ko alag-alag pehchaan paate hain.

PramanaFlood, The Tantric Body; Dyczkowski (Kubjika granthon par); Leadbeater, The Chakras.

72,000 nadi aur adhunik sharir ke bare mein savdhani.

72,000 nadi ek paramparik yogic varnan hai (Adhyay 20). Adhunik sharir-vigyan mein 'chakra' ya 'nadi' naam ki koi sanrachna nahi milti. Kuch log chakron ko endocrine glands ya nerve plexus se jodte hain, par yeh ek lokpriya tulna hai, siddh tathya nahi.

Dhyan rakhein

Kisi bhi sharirik ya mansik bimari ka ilaaj 'chakra-santulan' se karne ka daava na maanein. Bimari mein doctor se milein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Paramparik model aur adhunik chikitsa ko ek-doosre ki jagah na rakhein; dono apne apne dayre mein samajhe jaate hain.

PramanaSyllabus ka mool note; Flood; chikitsa-vigyan ki sarvamanya sthiti.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Saat-chakra naksha ka mukhya srot Shat-chakra-nirupana (Purnananda, lagbhag 1577 ee.) hai, jisme chhah chakra aur sahasrara aate hain.
  • Chakra ke dal 50 matrika se mel khate hain (4+6+10+12+16+2).
  • 'Chakra ke saath rang aur bimari' wali sarani aadhunik hai; mool granth mein nahi.
  • Chakra aur nadi paramparik models hain, adhunik sharir-vigyan ke tathya nahi.
← Adhyay 20Adhyay 22 →

PART VII · Sharir, yoga aur antar-sadhana

22

Yoga, dharana aur dhyan

योग, धारणा और ध्यान

Tantrik yoga ke saadhan, Hatha Yoga ki bhoomika aur Vijnana Bhairava ki dharana-parampara yahan saath mein padhi jaati hain, aur ek zaroori suraksha-sandesh ke saath.

Madhyam9 vishay

Asana, pranayama, puraka, rechaka, kumbhaka.

Asana baithne ya sharir ki sthiti ka abhyas hai. Patanjali ke anusaar asana sthir aur sukhakar hona chahiye (Yogasutra 2.46). Pranayama saans ka niyaman hai: puraka (saans lena), rechaka (chhodna) aur kumbhaka (rokna). Hatha Yoga Pradipika mein ashta-kumbhaka (aath prakar ke kumbhaka) ginaye gaye hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Kumbhaka (saans rokna) aur tez pranayama chakkar, behoshi aur ghabrahat paida kar sakte hain. Kisi bhi aise abhyas ko adhikari shikshak aur swasthya salah ke bina na karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh teen shabd saans ke vigyan ki buniyaad hain, aur Hatha Yoga ka mukhya vishay bhi.

PramanaYogasutra 2.46; Hatha Yoga Pradipika, adhyay 2.

Pratyahara, dharana, dhyan, samadhi.

Patanjali ke ashtanga-yoga ke aakhri char ang: pratyahara (indriyon ko bahar se samet lena), dharana (chitta ko ek jagah tikana), dhyana (us par nirantar dhyan) aur samadhi (poori lagan). Yogasutra ke teen sutra (3.1–3.3) inhe seedhe kram mein batate hain. Tantra mein bhi yehi shabd milte hain, par unke lakshya aur vyakhya sampraday-anusar badal jaate hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is shabdavali ko jaan lene se Patanjali, Hatha Yoga aur Tantra ki bhasha mein mel aur antar dikhne lagta hai.

PramanaYogasutra 2.29, 3.1–3.3.

Bandha, mudra aur shat-kriya ka textual context.

Hatha Yoga Pradipika ke teesre adhyay mein dus mudra aur bandha ginaye gaye hain (jaise maha-mudra, maha-bandha, maha-vedha, khechari, uddiyana, mula-bandha, jalandhara-bandha, viparita-karani, vajroli, shakti-chalana). Shat-kriya (shat-karma, chhah shuddhi-kriyaen) HYP 2.22 ke anusaar dhauti, basti, neti, trataka, nauli aur kapalabhati hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Yeh kriyayein (jaise vajroli, khechari ya dhauti) tej aur jokhim-bhari ho sakti hain. Isse sirf textual jaankari ke roop mein padhein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh suchi dikhati hai ki Hatha Yoga ka lakshya sirf aasan nahi, sharir ko shuddh aur sadhana ke liye taiyar karna bhi tha.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika 2.22, adhyay 3; Gheranda Samhita.

Trataka, nada-anusandhana, Soham, Hamsa, ajapa-japa.

Trataka ek vastu par ekagra drishti (aksar jyoti ya bindu par) ka abhyas hai. Nada-anusandhana bheetar ki dhwani par dhyan hai (HYP chauthe adhyay mein). Soham ('woh main hoon') aur Hamsa ek hi mantra ke ulte-seedhe roop hain, jo saans ke saath jode jaate hain, aur ajapa-japa wo japa hai jo bina bole saans ke saath chalti hai (Adhyay 12). Kai Nath granthon ke anusaar yeh din mein 21,600 baar swabhavik roop se hoti hai.

Dhyan rakhein

Trataka mein aankhon par tanav aa sakta hai; aankh ya mirgi ki samasya mein doctor se baat karein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Ye abhyas dikhate hain ki tantrik dhyan mein saans, dhwani aur dhyan ka ek saath kaam hota hai.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika (adhyay 1, 2, 4); Goraksha Shataka; Gheranda Samhita.

Yoga-nidra: historical aur modern forms.

Yoga-nidra ka shabd Puranon mein Vishnu ki 'yoga-nidra' (cosmic neend) ke roop mein aata hai, aur Devi Mahatmya mein Devi ko 'yoga-nidra' kaha gaya hai. Aaj ki lokpriya 'yoga-nidra' (leti hui, guided relaxation ki paddhati) 20vi sadi mein Swami Satyananda Saraswati ne lokpriya ki, jisne tantrik nyasa ki kuch paramparaon se prerna li.

Kyun / Aadhar

'Yoga-nidra' ke teen alag arth ko mat milayein: Puranik cosmic neend, tantrik dhyan-avastha, aur aaj ka guided relaxation.

PramanaDevi Mahatmya 1.x; Satyananda Saraswati ka yoga-nidra par kaam.

Visualisation, manasik puja aur antaryaga.

Tantrik dhyan mein mantra ya devata ka roop man mein dekhna ek aam tareeka hai, jise dhyana-shloka madad deta hai (Adhyay 17, Adhyay 13). Manasik puja aur antaryaga isi visualisation ka ritual roop hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse dikhta hai ki Tantra mein 'kalpana' ko sadhan maana gaya hai, jabki kuch dusre paramparaon mein use rukaavat maana jaata hai.

PramanaAbhinavagupta ke granth; Shri Vidya paddhati-granth.

Vijnana Bhairava dharana-tradition.

Vijnana Bhairava Tantra ek chhota granth hai (lagbhag 163 shloka) jisme lagbhag 112 dharana batayi jaati hain. Yeh Bhairava aur Bhairavi ke sanvad ke roop mein hai, jisme Devi puchhti hain ki param satya kya hai, aur Bhairava ek ke baad ek tareeke batate hain: saans ke do mode par dhyan (pehli dharana), do vichar ke beech ka antar, achanak ashchary ya khushi, vaasna ke ukhad jaane ka kshan, aadi. Jaideva Singh ne ise 7vi–8vi sadi ka maana hai; Abhinavagupta ise Shiva-jnana-upanishad kehte hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Is granth ki dharanayein bahut sankshipt hain; saans ya dhyan ki koi bhi dharana bina marg-darshan ke tez karne se bachein.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is granth ki khaas baat yeh hai ki yeh dhyan ko kisi ek mudra ya vidhi tak seemit nahi karta, balki roz ke anubhavon (saans, nazar, awaaz, ashchary) ko dhyan ka dwar maanta hai.

PramanaVijnana Bhairava Tantra (verses 24 aadi); Jaideva Singh, Vijnanabhairava or Divine Consciousness; Kshemaraja ki tika.

Hatha Yoga aur non-dual Shaiva meditation ke goals.

Hatha Yoga ka lakshya aksar sharir ko shuddh aur sthir karke prana ko sushumna mein le jaakar raja-yoga ya samadhi tak pahunchna hai. Non-dual Shaiva dhyan (Spanda, Pratyabhijna, Vijnana Bhairava) ka lakshya apni asli Shiva-chetna ki pehchan hai, jisme sharir ko badalne se zyada drishti ko badalne par zor hai. Dono ke saadhan aur lakshya ek-dusre se milte-julte hain par samaan nahi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is tulna se aap jaan sakte hain ki 'yoga' ek hi cheez nahi hai; har dhara ka lakshya aur tareeka alag hai.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika; Spanda Karika; Vijnana Bhairava Tantra.

Gentle guided practice aur intensive practice ka antar.

Ek saral dayra yeh ho sakta hai: halka (jaise sukhaasan mein baithna, saans par nazar rakhna, 5–10 minute ka dhyan, mantra ka kam-jaad japa) aur tez (jaise lambe kumbhaka, bahut ghante ki sadhana, upvaas, neend kam karna). Halke abhyas ke liye bhi shuruat mein ek anubhavi shikshak ya guru ka sath ho to behtar. Tez abhyas ke liye guru aur swasthya salah dono zaroori maane jaate hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Panic, behoshi, gambhir anidra, vichar-bhram ya lagataar disorientation ho to abhyas rok kar kisi qualified swasthya-vishesagya se baat karein. Bharat mein Tele-MANAS (14416) ek sarkari mansik-swasthya helpline hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh bhed isliye zaroori hai ki aksar log 'dhyan' ya 'kundalini' ka naam sunkar bina taiyari ke tez abhyas mein kood jaate hain.

PramanaHatha Yoga Pradipika 1.15 (atyahara aadi se bachne ki salah); swasthya-vishesagyon ki salah.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Yogasutra mein asana sthir aur sukhakar hona chahiye; pranayama puraka, rechaka aur kumbhaka se banta hai.
  • Hatha Yoga ke dus mudra-bandha HYP ke teesre adhyay mein, aur chhah shat-kriya HYP 2.22 mein ginaye gaye hain.
  • Vijnana Bhairava mein lagbhag 112 dharana hain; yeh dhyan ko roz ke anubhavon se jodta hai.
  • Tez abhyas bina guru aur swasthya salah ke na karein; takleef par abhyas rokein.
← Adhyay 21Adhyay 23 →

PART VIII · Devata, Devi aur upasana

23

Devata-svarupa aur iconography

देवता-स्वरूप और प्रतिमा-विज्ञान

Kisi bhi devata ko samajhne ke liye ek saaf study-template chahiye. Yeh adhyay woh template deta hai: naam, tattva, roop, shakti, parivar, kshetriya roop, aur ek devata ke alag granthon mein alag varnan.

Basic6 vishay

Naam, tattva, theological position aur dhyana-shloka.

Har devata ke liye pehle poochhein: unka naam kya hai aur uska kya arth hai (nirukti)? Wo tattva ke roop mein kaun hain (param tatva, ek roop, ya ek shakti)? Sampraday mein unka sthan kya hai? Aur unka dhyana-shloka kya hai, yaani woh shloka jo unka roop man mein banata hai. Udaharan: Vishnu ka dhyana 'shantakaram bhujagashayanam' wala shloka lokpriya hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Naam, tattva aur dhyana-shloka teen mil kar devata ki 'pehchaan-patra' bante hain.

PramanaPaddhati-granth; Purana.

Roop, bhujayein, mudra, ayudha, vahana, asana, vastra.

Devata ki iconography ke ang: roop (varn, mukh, netra), bhuja (haath ki sankhya), mudra (jaise abhaya ya varada), ayudha (astra ya prateek), vahana (sawari), asana (baithne ki mudra) aur vastra. Har ang ka apna sanket hota hai: jaise Durga ke astra alag-alag devataon se prapt maane jaate hain (Devi Mahatmya).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse murti ya chitra ko 'padha' ja sakta hai: har cheez ka arth hota hai.

PramanaDevi Mahatmya; Vishnudharmottara Purana; Agama ke murti-lakshana prakaran.

Shakti/consort aur parivara-devata.

Aksar ek devata ke saath unki Shakti ya patni aur parivar hota hai: Shiva ke saath Parvati, Ganesha aur Kartikeya; Vishnu ke saath Lakshmi; Surya ke saath Samjna aur Chhaya aadi. Tantra mein yeh Shakti khud ek devi ke roop mein upasana ka kendra ban sakti hain (Adhyay 5).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki devata akele nahi, ek poori vyavastha ke bheetar hote hain.

PramanaPurana; Agama.

Regional forms, mantra, stotra, sahasranama aur kavacha.

Ek hi devata alag kshetra mein alag roop aur naam se milta hai: Shiva ke Kerala, Kashmir aur Tamil Nadu ke alag roop; Devi ke Bengal (Durga, Kali), Assam (Kamakhya), Tamil Nadu (Meenakshi) roop; Vishnu ke Venkateshvara, Jagannatha aadi roop. Har roop ke apne mantra, stotra, sahasranama aur kavacha ho sakte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh vividhta dikhati hai ki 'ek devata' ek hi chhavi mein nahi bandha, aur yeh bhi ki lok aur shastra ek-doosre ko prabhavit karte hain.

PramanaSthala-purana; kshetriya mandir-parampara.

Purana, Agama, Tantra aur stotra mein ek devata ke variants.

Ek hi devata ka varnan Purana mein katha-roop mein, Agama mein murti-lakshana ke roop mein, Tantra mein mantra-yantra ke roop mein aur stotra mein bhakti-roop mein milta hai, aur in mein antar ho sakta hai. Udaharan: Ganesha ka varnan Ganesha Purana, Mudgala Purana aur Ganapati Atharvashirsha mein alag zor ke saath aata hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye 'sahi roop' ek nahi hota; granth ke prakar ke hisaab se roop badalta hai.

PramanaGanesha Purana; Mudgala Purana; Ganapati Atharvashirsha.

Iconography aur ritual worship ka sambandh.

Iconography puja ko sidhi disha deti hai: dhyana-shloka murti ko man mein banata hai, murti puja ka kendra banti hai, aur puja ke upachara devata ke gun aur roop ke anusar badal sakte hain (Adhyay 17, Adhyay 19).

Kyun / Aadhar

Isse samajh aata hai ki murti sirf kala nahi, ek upasana-vastu hai.

PramanaAgama; Paddhati-granth.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Har devata ke liye naam, tattva, roop, shakti, kshetriya roop aur granth-bhed ka ek card banayein.
  • Ek devata Purana, Agama, Tantra aur stotra mein alag tarah se aata hai.
  • Iconography ke har ang (mudra, ayudha, vahana) ka sanket hota hai.
  • Murti, mantra aur yantra ek hi devata ke teen roop hain.
← Adhyay 22Adhyay 24 →

PART VIII · Devata, Devi aur upasana

24

Dash Mahavidya

दश महाविद्या

Dash Mahavidya, yaani Devi ke das gyaan-roop, Shakta paramparaon ka ek prasiddh samuh hai. Yahan unka kram, roop, dharmik bhoomika aur granth-aadhar ek study-file ke roop mein diye gaye hain.

Madhyam5 vishay

Dash Mahavidya ki suchi aur origin.

Kram (aam suchi): 1. Kali, 2. Tara, 3. Tripura Sundari (Shodashi), 4. Bhuvaneshvari, 5. Bhairavi, 6. Chhinnamasta, 7. Dhumavati, 8. Bagalamukhi, 9. Matangi, 10. Kamala.

Kai granthon (jaise Mahabhagavata Purana) mein ek katha milti hai: Sati ko Shiva ne Daksha ke yajna mein jaane se roka, to krodh mein Sati ne das roop dikhaye jisse Shiva ghire. Yeh ek hi katha-roop hai; alag granth alag tareeke se in devi-roopon ka mool batate hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Suchi aur kram bhi sabhi granthon mein ek jaisa nahi milta. Ek granth mein kuch naam alag ho sakte hain.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh suchi Rudrayamala, Tantrasara (Abhinavagupta), Devi Bhagavata, Kalika Purana, Brahmanda Purana aur anya granthon mein alag-alag roop mein aati hai.

PramanaMahabhagavata Purana; Kinsley, Tantric Visions of the Divine Feminine; Wikipedia, Mahavidyas (suchi aur granth-sandarbh).

Dash Mahavidya: ek nazar mein study-table.

MahavidyaMukhya roop (iconography)Mukhya pehchaan
KaliGehra kala varn, teen netra, lal jibha, mund-mala, chaar hath (talwar, trishul, asur-sir, patra)Samay ya kal ki avastha, srishti aur samhara, mukti
TaraHalka neela varn, gale mein sarp, chandra ka mukut, chaar hath (kamal, khadga, asur-sir, kainchi), Shiva par pair'Tarane wali', sankat se paar karne wali; Buddhist Tara se bhi jodi jaati hai
Tripura Sundari (Shodashi)Tapte sone ka varn, teen shant netra, chaar hath (ankusha, kamal, dhanush, baan), singhasan parSaundarya aur param tatva; Shri Vidya ki kendra devi
BhuvaneshvariGora aur sunahri varn, chaar hath (ankusha, pasha aur do khule), singhasan par'Bhuvan ki ishvari'; srishti ka aakash-tatva
BhairaviLal varn, teen krodhit netra, sarp ke abhushan, mala aur pustak liyeTapasya ki ugra shakti; kai vyakhyaon mein vidya aur vairagya
ChhinnamastaLal varn, apna kata hua sir haath mein, talwar aur anya, ek yugal ke upar khadiAtma-balidan ka prateek, jeevan-mrityu ka chakra
DhumavatiDhoomil, jhurriyon wali, safed vidhava-vastra, lal aankhein, kauve ke dhwaj wala ghodarahit rathAlakshmi, ashanti aur vairagya; sukh ke baad ki khaali jagah
BagalamukhiSunahri varn, peela vastra, gada liye, rakshas ki jibha pakadti'Stambhana' (rok dene ki shakti); vani ka sanyam
MatangiPrayah hara varn (kabhi neela), lal vastra, veena, khadga aur kapal liyeVani, sangeet aur kala; ucchishta ki sankalpana se bhi jodi jaati hai
KamalaSone ka varn, kamal par baithi, do hath mein kamal aur do hath mein varada aur abhayaLakshmi ke roop mein dhan, saundarya aur sukh
Dhyan rakhein

Kai varnan (jaise Kali, Chhinnamasta, Dhumavati) ugra hain aur unka arth prateekatmak mana jaata hai. Yeh sirf iconography ka parichay hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is table se har devi ki chhavi aur uska sanketik arth ek nazar mein dikhta hai.

PramanaWikipedia, Mahavidyas (iconography); Kinsley, Tantric Visions of the Divine Feminine.

Har Mahavidya ke liye study-file: kya-kya dekhein.

Har Mahavidya ke liye ek file banayein, jisme ye khane hon: naam aur uski vyutpatti, granthon mein sandarbh, iconography, dhyana, dharmik bhoomika, stotra, kavacha aur sahasranama, kshetriya roop, Shakta paramparaon mein sthan, aitihasik vikas, daave aur saboot ka antar, aur sadharan puja aur diksha-yukt sadhana ka antar.

Kyun / Aadhar

Is tarah ki file bhi aapko yaad dilati hai ki har devi ke bare mein 'kya granth mein hai' aur 'kya sirf lokpriya kahani hai' alag rakhna zaroori hai.

PramanaSyllabus ka mool nirdesh.

Shakta context aur aitihasik vikas.

Mahavidya ke naam ke saath dus-devi ka ek samuh madhyakaal mein, kai sthanon mein, aur Bengal aur Assam ke Shakta kshetron mein vishesh roop se lokpriya hua. Kinsley ke anusaar yeh Shakta vichar ka ek vishesh dhara hai, jisme alag-alag devi-roop ek hi param Shakti ke alag pehlu maane gaye hain. Kamakhya (Assam) mein dus Mahavidya ke alag mandir hain, aur Tarapith (West Bengal) Tara ka prasiddh sthan hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh dikhata hai ki Mahavidya samuh ek hi samay mein, ek hi granth se nahi banta; usme sadiyon ka vikas hai.

PramanaKinsley; Mahabhagavata Purana; kshetriya sthala-parampara.

Sadharan puja aur diksha-yukt sadhana ka antar.

Kuch devi-roop mandir mein khulkar poojit hote hain: Kali, Tara aur Kamala jaise roop ki puja aam taur par ghar aur mandir mein dekhi jaati hai. Doosre roop, jaise Chhinnamasta ya Dhumavati, ki upasana aksar zyada guru-nirdeshit aur kam sarvajanik maani jaati hai. Bagalamukhi ke bhi kuch prasiddh mandir (jaise Datia aur Nalkheda) hain, jahan loga darshan karte hain.

Dhyan rakhein

Iska arth yeh nahi ki kisi roop ki upasana 'badi' ya 'chhoti' hai; sirf yeh ki alag roop ke liye alag parampara ke niyam hain. Bagalamukhi ya Chhinnamasta ke naam par 'vashikaran' ya 'sauda' bechne wale daavon se bachein (Adhyay 29).

Kyun / Aadhar

Yeh samajhne se aap darshan, bhakti aur guru-nirdeshit sadhana ke antar ko pehchan paate hain.

PramanaKinsley; sthaniya mandir-parampara.

Yaad rakhne ke points

  • Dash Mahavidya ki aam suchi: Kali, Tara, Tripura Sundari, Bhuvaneshvari, Bhairavi, Chhinnamasta, Dhumavati, Bagalamukhi, Matangi, Kamala.
  • Origin katha (Sati aur Daksha) ek hi roop hai; alag granth alag tarah se batate hain.
  • Iconography ugra ho to bhi uska arth prateekatmak mana jaata hai.
  • Sadharan puja aur diksha-yukt sadhana alag cheezein hain.
← Adhyay 23Adhyay 25 →

PART VIII · Devata, Devi aur upasana

25

Mahavidya se pare Devi-parampara

महाविद्या से परे देवी-परम्परा

Shakta duniya Dash Mahavidya se kahin badi hai. Durga aur Devi Mahatmya, Lalita, Kubjika, gram-devi, Kamakhya aur Puranic Devi paramparayein yahan ek saath rakhi gayi hain.

Madhyam6 vishay

Durga, Navadurga, Chandika aur Devi Mahatmya.

Devi Mahatmya (Durga Saptashati) Markandeya Purana ka ek ansh hai (adhyay 81–93), jisme lagbhag 700 shloka aur 13 adhyay hain. Isme teen mukhya kathayein hain: Madhu-Kaitabha ka vadh, Mahishasura ka vadh aur Shumbha-Nishumbha ka vadh. Devi yahan sabhi devataon ki sammilit shakti ke roop mein prakat hoti hain, aur ek hi param Shakti ke roop mein bhi varnit hain.

Navadurga Navaratri ke nau dino ki nau roop hain: Shailaputri, Brahmacharini, Chandraghanta, Kushmanda, Skandamata, Katyayani, Kalaratri, Mahagauri aur Siddhidatri. Chandika Devi Mahatmya mein Durga ka ek prasiddh naam hai.

Kyun / Aadhar

Devi Mahatmya Shakta bhakti ka sabse prasiddh Puranic granth hai, aur Shakta Tantra ki bahut si paramparayein ise apna aadhar maanti hain.

PramanaDevi Mahatmya (Markandeya Purana 81–93); Navaratri ki paramparik suchi.

Lalita Tripurasundari, Bhuvaneshvari aur Rajarajeshvari.

Lalita Tripurasundari Shri Vidya ki kendra devi hain (Adhyay 27). Rajarajeshvari ('rajaon ki bhi raja ishvari') unka ek aur prasiddh naam hai. Bhuvaneshvari ka sthan Dash Mahavidya mein bhi hai aur Shakta bhakti mein bhi. Tantra mein in teeno naam ke beech sambandh sampraday ke hisaab se badalta hai: kahin yeh ek hi devi ke naam hain, kahin alag devi-roop.

Kyun / Aadhar

Isliye ek hi naam ko sabhi granthon mein ek hi devi se jodna sahi nahi; sampraday dekhna zaroori hai.

PramanaLalita Sahasranama; Shri Vidya granth; Devi Bhagavata Purana.

Kubjika, Yogini traditions, grama-devata aur regional Shakti-upasana.

Kubjika dhara aur yogini-paramparayein pehle (Adhyay 3) aayi hain. Grama-devata gaon ki rakshak devi-devata hain: Tamil Nadu mein Mariamman, Uttar Bharat mein Sitala, Maharashtra mein Yellamma aur Mhasoba, aur anya kshetron mein kai aur. Inki puja ke tareeke aur kathayein sthaniya hoti hain aur aksar mukhya dhara se alag bhi.

Kyun / Aadhar

Grama-devata ki upasana yeh batati hai ki Shakti-bhakti sirf granthon mein nahi, gaon ke roz ke jeevan mein jeevit hai.

PramanaDyczkowski (Kubjika); Elmore aur anya lok-dharmik shodh.
